    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2418, , "Íå ñîçäàíà íîâàÿ òàáëèöà ÎÊ1."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, COL2_W, 0
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 1, COL3_W, 0
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 2, COL4_W, 0
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 3, COL5_W, 0
    ' Ôîðìàòèðóåì äî îáúåäèíåíèÿ, ÷òîáû íå îáðàùàòüñÿ ê ïîãëîù¸ííûì ÿ÷åéêàì.
    If Not ApplyOperationTableFont(tbl) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2419, , "Íå çàäàí øðèôò ÎÊ1."
    For r = 0 To OK1_DATA_ROWS - 1
        tbl.SetRowHeight r, ROW_H, 0
        For c = 0 To NUM_COLS - 1
            If c < 2 Then
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(r, c) = 1
            Else
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(r, c) = 2
            End If
        Next c
    Next r
    For r = 0 To 2
        ' Òðè íåçàâèñèìûõ îáúåäèíåíèÿ ïî ãîðèçîíòàëè, íå îäíî âåðòèêàëüíîå.
        merged = tbl.MergeCells(r, 0, r, NUM_COLS - 1)
        If Not merged Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2420, , _
            "Íå îáúåäèíåíû ÿ÷åéêè ñòðîêè " & CStr(r + 1) & " íà ÎÊ1."
    Next r
    For r = 0 To OK1_DATA_ROWS - 1
        If r < 3 Then
            tbl.Text2(r, 0, False) = data(r, 0)
        Else
            For c = 0 To NUM_COLS - 1
                tbl.Text2(r, c, False) = data(r, c)
            Next c
        End If
        tbl.SetRowHeight r, ROW_H, 0
    Next r
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = -1
    tbl.GridLineWeight = -1
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2421, , "Íåò àííîòàöèè òàáëèöû ÎÊ1."
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(OK1_TABLE_X, OK1_TABLE_Y, 0) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2422, , "Íå çàäàíà ïîçèöèÿ òàáëèöû ÎÊ1."
    If tbl.rowCount <> OK1_DATA_ROWS Or tbl.ColumnCount <> NUM_COLS Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2423, , "Íåâåðíîå êîëè÷åñòâî ñòðîê èëè ñòîëáöîâ ÎÊ1."
    For r = 0 To OK1_DATA_ROWS - 1
        If Abs(tbl.GetRowHeight(r) - ROW_H) > 0.00005 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2424, , _
            "Ïîñëå èçìåðåíèÿ ïåðåíîñà ôàêòè÷åñêàÿ âûñîòà ñòðîêè " & CStr(r + 1) & _
            " ÎÊ1 ðàâíà " & Format$(tbl.GetRowHeight(r) * 1000#, "0.000") & " ìì âìåñòî 8,5." & _
            " Ïðîâåðüòå øðèôò/ïîëÿ ÿ÷ååê. Ðàáî÷àÿ òàáëèöà íå çàìåíåíà."
        For c = 0 To NUM_COLS - 1
            If r >= 3 Or c = 0 Then
                If FrameTextComparable(CStr(tbl.Text2(r, c, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(data(r, c)) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2425, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í òåêñò íîâîé òàáëèöû ÎÊ1."
            End If
        Next c
    Next r
    ' Ñíà÷àëà ñòðîèì è ïðîâåðÿåì íîâóþ òàáëèöó. Ïðåæíþþ íå óäàëÿåì ïðè îøèáêå ïîñòðîåíèÿ.
    Set feat = GetFrameTableFeature(tbl)
    If Not oldTable Is Nothing Then
        Set oldFeature = GetFrameTableFeature(oldTable)
        oldName = oldFeature.name
        oldFeature.name = "MF_OK1_DATA_BACKUP_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss")
        If StrComp(oldFeature.name, oldName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2430, , "Íå îñâîáîæäåíî èìÿ ïðåæíåé òàáëèöû ÎÊ1."
        oldRenamed = True
    End If
    feat.name = "Òàáëèöà äàííûõ1"
    If StrComp(feat.name, "Òàáëèöà äàííûõ1", vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2426, , _
        "Íå ïðèñâîåíî èìÿ 'Òàáëèöà äàííûõ1'. Ïðîâåðüòå, íå çàíÿòî ëè îíî íà äðóãîì ëèñòå."
    If Not oldTable Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate swModel, oldTable
    complete = True
    Set frameAttributeNames = Nothing
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number
    errorText = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not complete Then
        If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate swModel, tbl
        If oldRenamed Then
            oldFeature.name = oldName
            If Err.number <> 0 Then errorText = errorText & _
                " Íå óäàëîñü âåðíóòü èìÿ ïðåæíåé òàáëèöû. Ïðîâåðüòå MF_OK1_DATA_BACKUP_ íà ÎÊ1."
        End If
    End If
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "BuildOK1DataTable", errorText
End Sub

Private Sub CheckOK1LayoutAfterSeamUpdate(ByVal swDraw As Object)
    Dim tbl As Object, r As Long
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, "ÎÊ1") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2432, , "Íå àêòèâèðîâàí ÎÊ1 äëÿ ïðîâåðêè âûñîòû ñòðîê."
    Set tbl = GetTableByName(swDraw, "Òàáëèöà äàííûõ1")
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2433, , "Ïîñëå çàïîëíåíèÿ íå íàéäåíà òàáëèöà ÎÊ1."
    For r = 0 To OK1_DATA_ROWS - 1
        tbl.SetRowHeight r, ROW_H, 0
        If Abs(tbl.GetRowHeight(r) - ROW_H) > 0.00005 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2434, , _
            "Ïîñëå îáíîâëåíèÿ íîìåðîâ øâîâ ñòðîêà " & CStr(r + 1) & _
            " ÎÊ1 íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ â 8,5 ìì. Ñîêðàòèòå òåêñò ñîîòâåòñòâóþùåé ñòðîêè â Excel."
    Next r
End Sub

Private Sub NormalizeIntroBlocks(ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    Dim buffer() As Variant, starts() As Long, stops() As Long, lengths() As Long
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, groups As Long, g As Long, lastUsed As Long
    Dim outputCount As Long, outRow As Long, anyValue As Boolean
    If introCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim starts(1 To introCount)
    ReDim stops(1 To introCount)
    ReDim lengths(1 To introCount)
    For r = 1 To introCount
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            If Not IsIntroOperationKey(CStr(intro(r, 1))) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2600, , "Â ââîäíûõ îïåðàöèÿõ íàéäåí íåèçâåñòíûé èäåíòèôèêàòîð."
            If groups > 0 Then stops(groups) = r - 1
            groups = groups + 1
            starts(groups) = r
        ElseIf groups = 0 Then
            For c = 2 To 5
                If IsError(intro(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, , "Îøèáêà Excel âî ââîäíîé îïåðàöèè."
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, c)))) > 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2602, , "Ââîäíàÿ îïåðàöèÿ äîëæíà íà÷èíàòüñÿ ñ ìåòêè ÎÊ1-... â A."
            Next c
        End If
    Next r
    If groups = 0 Then
        introCount = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    stops(groups) = introCount
    For g = 1 To groups
        lastUsed = starts(g)
        For r = starts(g) To stops(g)
            anyValue = False
            For c = 1 To 5
                If IsError(intro(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2603, , "Îøèáêà Excel âî ââîäíîé îïåðàöèè."
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, c)))) > 0 Then anyValue = True
            Next c
            If anyValue Then lastUsed = r
        Next r
        lengths(g) = lastUsed - starts(g) + 1
        If lengths(g) < 4 Then lengths(g) = 4
        outputCount = outputCount + lengths(g)
        stops(g) = lastUsed
    Next g
    ReDim buffer(1 To outputCount, 1 To 5)
    outRow = 1
    For g = 1 To groups
        For r = starts(g) To stops(g)
            For c = 1 To 5
                buffer(outRow + r - starts(g), c) = intro(r, c)
            Next c
        Next r
        outRow = outRow + lengths(g)
    Next g
    ReDim intro(1 To outputCount, 1 To 5)
    For r = 1 To outputCount
        For c = 1 To 5
            intro(r, c) = buffer(r, c)
        Next c
    Next r
    introCount = outputCount
End Sub

Private Function EnsureCardToolsList(ByVal book As Object) As Boolean
    Dim nm As Object, rng As Object, ws As Object, candidate As Object
    Dim tools As Variant, i As Long, lastRow As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = book.names("ToolsList")
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then Set rng = nm.RefersToRange
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not rng Is Nothing Then
        If rng.Columns.count = 1 Then
            If rng.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(rng) > 0 Then Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    For Each candidate In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(candidate.name, "ÑïèñîêÒÎ", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set ws = candidate
            Exit For
        End If
    Next candidate
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
        ws.name = "ÑïèñîêÒÎ"
    End If
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("A1:A" & CStr(lastRow))) = 0 Then
        tools = Array("Øàáëîí ñâàðùèêà ÓØÑ-2", "Øàáëîí ñâàðùèêà ÓØÑ ÌÃ-8", "Âèçóàëüíî", "Ðóëåòêà Ð5Ó2Ï ÃÎÑÒ 7502-98", _
            "Óãîëüíèê ïîâåðî÷íûé ñëåñàðíûé ÃÎÑÒ 7502-77", "Ëóïà ÃÎÑÒ 25706-83", "ÊÈÌ", _
            "Ëèíåéêà ØÄ-1-1000 ÃÎÑÒ 8026-92", "Íàáîð ùóïîâ ÃÎÑÒ 882-75", _
            "Øòàíãåíöèðêóëü ØÖ-I-125-0,05 ÃÎÑÒ 166-89", "Ïðàâèëî", "Óðîâåíü ñòðîèòåëüíûé ÃÎÑÒ 9416-83", _
            "Êëåéìî ÎÒÊ", "Ìîëîòîê 7850-0103 ÃÎÑÒ 2310-77")
        For i = 0 To UBound(tools)
            ws.cells(i + 1, 1).Value2 = tools(i)
        Next i
        lastRow = UBound(tools) + 1
    End If
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then nm.Delete
    book.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(lastRow)
    ws.Visible = False
    EnsureCardToolsList = True
End Function

Private Function IsCardEndRow(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal r As Long) As Boolean
    Dim a As String, b As String, c As String
    a = UCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 1))
    b = LCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 2))
    c = LCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 3))
    If a = "ÎÊ" Or a = "OK" Then
        IsCardEndRow = True
    ElseIf InStr(1, b & " " & c, "îïåðàöèîííàÿ êàðòà êîíòðîëÿ", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        IsCardEndRow = True
    End If
End Function

Private Function NeedsCardToolValidation(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, tp As Long, formula As String, dropDown As Boolean
    lastRow = LastOKContinuationRow(ws)
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        If IsCardEndRow(ws, r) Then Exit For
        tp = 0
        formula = ""
        dropDown = False
        On Error Resume Next
        tp = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.Type
        formula = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.Formula1
        dropDown = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.InCellDropdown
        On Error GoTo 0
        If tp <> 3 Or StrComp(formula, "=ToolsList", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Or Not dropDown Then
            NeedsCardToolValidation = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
End Function

Private Sub FormatCardOperationBlock(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal firstRow As Long, ByVal lastRow As Long)
    Dim r As Long, block As Object
    Set block = ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":E" & CStr(lastRow))
    block.Font.Bold = False
    block.Interior.Pattern = -4142
    block.VerticalAlignment = -4160
    ws.Range("B" & CStr(firstRow) & ":C" & CStr(lastRow)).WrapText = True
    ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":A" & CStr(lastRow)).HorizontalAlignment = -4108
    ws.Range("D" & CStr(firstRow) & ":E" & CStr(lastRow)).HorizontalAlignment = -4108
    For r = firstRow To lastRow
        If r = firstRow Then
            ws.rows(r).RowHeight = 30
        Else
            ws.rows(r).RowHeight = 22
        End If
        With ws.cells(r, 3).Validation
            .Delete
            .Add Type:=3, AlertStyle:=2, Operator:=1, Formula1:="=ToolsList"
            .IgnoreBlank = True
            .InCellDropdown = True
            .ShowError = True
        End With
    Next r
    block.Borders(9).LineStyle = 1
    block.Borders(9).Weight = 2
    block.Borders(12).LineStyle = 2
    block.Borders(12).Weight = 1
    block.Borders(12).Color = RGB(180, 180, 180)
End Sub

Private Sub ApplyCardOperationLayout(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim r As Long, first As Long, lastRow As Long, dataEnd As Long
    lastRow = LastOKContinuationRow(ws)
    dataEnd = lastRow
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        If IsCardEndRow(ws, r) Then
            dataEnd = r - 1
            Exit For
        End If
    Next r
    For r = 7 To dataEnd
        If Len(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 1)) > 0 Then
            If first > 0 Then FormatCardOperationBlock ws, first, r - 1
            first = r
        End If
    Next r
    If first > 0 Then FormatCardOperationBlock ws, first, dataEnd
End Sub

' Èçìåðÿåì ïåðåíîñ ñàìèì SolidWorks, à íå òîëüêî êîëè÷åñòâîì ñèìâîëîâ.
Private Function ApplyOperationTableFont(ByVal tbl As Object) As Boolean
    Dim r As Long, c As Long
    If operationTableFormat Is Nothing Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2610, , "Íå ïîäãîòîâëåí ôîðìàò òàáëèö îïåðàöèé."
    If Not tbl.SetTextFormat(False, operationTableFormat) Then Exit Function
    For r = 0 To tbl.rowCount - 1
        For c = 0 To tbl.ColumnCount - 1
            If Not tbl.SetCellTextFormat(r, c, False, operationTableFormat) Then Exit Function
        Next c
    Next r
    ApplyOperationTableFont = True
End Function

Private Function CreateWrapProbe(ByVal model As Object, Optional ByVal fullWidth As Boolean = False) As Object
    Dim tbl As Object, n As Long, en As Long, et As String, widths As Variant, c As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    n = NUM_COLS
    If fullWidth Then n = 1
    Set tbl = model.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation(False, -0.4, -0.4, 1, "", 1, n)
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2611, , "Íå ñîçäàíà âðåìåííàÿ òàáëèöà èçìåðåíèÿ ïåðåíîñà."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    If fullWidth Then
        tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, COL2_W + COL3_W + COL4_W + COL5_W, 0
        If Abs(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) - (COL2_W + COL3_W + COL4_W + COL5_W)) > 0.00005 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2620, , "Íå çàäàíà ïîëíàÿ øèðèíà âðåìåííîé òàáëèöû."
    Else
        tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, COL2_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth 1, COL3_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth 2, COL4_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth 3, COL5_W, 0
        widths = Array(COL2_W, COL3_W, COL4_W, COL5_W)
        For c = 0 To 3
            If Abs(tbl.GetColumnWidth(c) - CDbl(widths(c))) > 0.00005 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2620, , "Íå çàäàíû øèðèíû ñòîëáöîâ âðåìåííîé òàáëèöû."
        Next c
    End If
    If operationTableFormat Is Nothing Then
        If Not ApplyMacroTableFont(tbl) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2612, , "Íå çàäàí øðèôò âðåìåííîé òàáëèöû."
        Set operationTableFormat = tbl.GetTextFormat
    End If
    If Not ApplyOperationTableFont(tbl) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2613, , "Íå çàäàí åäèíûé ôîðìàò èçìåðåíèÿ."
    tbl.SetRowHeight 0, ROW_H, 0
    If tbl.GetRowHeight(0) > ROW_H + 0.00005 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2614, , _
        "Äàæå ïóñòàÿ ñòðîêà òàáëèöû âûøå 8,5 ìì. Ïðîâåðüòå íàñòðîéêè øðèôòà è ïîëåé ÿ÷ååê äîêóìåíòà."
    Set CreateWrapProbe = tbl
    Exit Function
Failed:
    en = Err.number
    et = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise en, "CreateWrapProbe", et
End Function

Private Sub DropWrapProbe(ByVal model As Object, ByRef probe As Object)
    If Not probe Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, probe
    Set probe = Nothing
End Sub

Private Function ProbeCellFits(ByVal probe As Object, ByVal col As Long, ByVal text As String) As Boolean
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To probe.ColumnCount - 1
        probe.Text2(0, c, False) = ""
    Next c
    probe.Text2(0, col, False) = text
    probe.SetRowHeight 0, ROW_H, 0
    ProbeCellFits = probe.GetRowHeight(0) <= ROW_H + 0.00005
End Function

Private Function FitPrefix(ByVal text As String, ByVal maxChars As Long, _
    ByVal col As Long, ByVal probe As Object) As String
    Dim lo As Long, hi As Long, pivot As Long, candidate As String, best As String, lines As Variant
    If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, text) Then
        FitPrefix = text
        Exit Function
    End If
    lo = 1
    hi = Len(text) - 1
    If hi > maxChars Then hi = maxChars
    ' Îáû÷íûé ïóòü: ñîõðàíÿåì ïðåæíþþ ëîãèêó ñëîâ, ïðåäëîãîâ è ñâÿçàííûõ îáîçíà÷åíèé.
    Do While lo <= hi
        pivot = (lo + hi) \ 2
        lines = WrapTextByWords(text, pivot)
        candidate = CStr(lines(LBound(lines)))
        If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, candidate) Then
            If Len(candidate) > Len(best) Then best = candidate
            lo = pivot + 1
        Else
            hi = pivot - 1
        End If
    Loop
    If Len(best) = 0 Then
        ' Î÷åíü äëèííîå íåäåëèìîå ñëîâî/îáîçíà÷åíèå: äåëèì áåç óäàëåíèÿ ñèìâîëîâ.
        lo = 1
        hi = Len(text) - 1
        Do While lo <= hi
            pivot = (lo + hi) \ 2
            candidate = Left$(text, pivot)
            If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, candidate) Then
                best = candidate
                lo = pivot + 1
            Else
                hi = pivot - 1
            End If
        Loop
    End If
    If Len(best) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2615, , _
        "Â ÿ÷åéêó íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ äàæå îäèí ñèìâîë. Ïðîâåðüòå øðèôò è ïîëÿ ÿ÷åéêè."
    If StrComp(Left$(text, Len(best)), best, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2616, , "Íå ñîâïàë èñõîäíûé òåêñò ïðè ðàñ÷¸òå ïåðåíîñà."
    FitPrefix = best
End Function

Private Function WrapTextForTable(ByVal text As String, ByVal maxChars As Long, ByVal col As Long, ByVal probe As Object) As Variant
    Dim paragraphs As Variant, paragraph As Variant, rest As String, n As Long, count As Long, output() As String
    ' maxChars îñòàâëåí äëÿ ñîâìåñòèìîñòè âûçîâîâ; ôàêòè÷åñêóþ âìåñòèìîñòü èçìåðÿåò SolidWorks.
    text = KC14_ProtectedText(text)
    ' Â VBA Split("", vbLf) íå äà¸ò íè îäíîãî ýëåìåíòà. Ïîýòîìó öèêë íå
    ' èíèöèàëèçèðîâàë output(), à UBound âûçûâàë îøèáêó 9. Ïóñòàÿ êîëîíêà äîïóñòèìà.
    If Len(text) = 0 Then
        ReDim output(1 To 1)
        output(1) = ""
        WrapTextForTable = output
        Exit Function
    End If
    paragraphs = Split(text, vbLf)
    For Each paragraph In paragraphs
        rest = Trim$(CStr(paragraph))
        If Len(rest) = 0 Then
            count = count + 1: ReDim Preserve output(1 To count): output(count) = ""
        End If
        Do While Len(rest) > 0
            n = KC14_Fit(rest, col, probe)
            count = count + 1: ReDim Preserve output(1 To count)
            output(count) = Replace(Left$(rest, n), Chr(1), " ")
            rest = LTrim$(Mid$(rest, n + 1))
        Loop
    Next paragraph
    WrapTextForTable = output
End Function

Private Sub ValidateFixedOK1Text(ByVal model As Object, ByRef data() As String)
    Dim probe As Object, r As Long, en As Long, et As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set probe = CreateWrapProbe(model, True)
    For r = 0 To 2
        If Not ProbeCellFits(probe, 0, data(r, 0)) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2617, , _
            "Ôèêñèðîâàííàÿ ñòðîêà ÎÊ1 ¹" & CStr(r + 1) & " íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ â îäíó ñòðîêó 8,5 ìì ïîëíîé øèðèíû ÎÊ1."
    Next r
    DropWrapProbe model, probe
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    en = Err.number
    et = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    DropWrapProbe model, probe
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise en, "ValidateFixedOK1Text", et
End Sub

' Èçìåíåíèå êîëè÷åñòâà ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ òðåáóåò ïîâòîðíîé ïåðåíóìåðàöèè áàëëîíîâ,
' à íå òîëüêî âñòàâêè ñòðîê â ãîòîâûé Excel.
Private Sub KC_ValidateCardNumbering(ByVal book As Object, ByVal card As Object)
    Dim raw As Object, sheet As Object, introCount As Long, rawCount As Long
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String, n As Double, expected As Long, seen As Long
    Dim regularStarted As Boolean, rx As Object, matches As Object, text As String
    lastRow = LastOKContinuationRow(card)
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        key = OK1ExcelText(card, r, 1)
        If Len(key) > 0 Then
            If KC15_IsIntroRow(card, r) Then
                If regularStarted Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2751, , "Ââîäíûå ïóíêòû äîëæíû èäòè äî ïàðàìåòðîâ ÷åðòåæà."
                introCount = introCount + 1
            Else
                regularStarted = True
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    For Each sheet In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(sheet.name, "Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set raw = sheet
    Next sheet
    If raw Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2752, , _
        "Íåò ëèñòà 'Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà': íåâîçìîæíî ïðîâåðèòü íîìåðà áàëëîíîâ. Íóæåí ïîëíûé ýêñïîðò èç ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà (ðåæèì 1)."
    If OK1ExcelText(raw, 1, 1) <> "¹ ïîç." Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2753, , "Èçìåíåíà øàïêà ëèñòà 'Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà'."
    For r = 2 To raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        key = OK1ExcelText(raw, r, 1)
        If Len(key) > 0 Then
            expected = introCount + rawCount + 1
            If Not NumericCellValue(key, n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2754, , "Íå÷èñëîâîé íîìåð ïîçèöèè â èñõîäíûõ äàííûõ."
            If n <> expected Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2755, , _
                "Êîëè÷åñòâî ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ íå ñîãëàñîâàíî ñ áàëëîíàìè. Îæèäàåòñÿ ïîçèöèÿ " & CStr(expected) & ", ïîëó÷åíî " & key & ". Ïîâòîðèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà ñ íóæíûì ñïèñêîì."
            rawCount = rawCount + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If rawCount = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2756, , "Íåò ýêñïîðòèðîâàííûõ ïîçèöèé ÷åðòåæà."
    Set rx = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    rx.Pattern = "^\s*([0-9]+)[.)]\s*"
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        key = OK1ExcelText(card, r, 1)
        If Len(key) > 0 And Not KC15_IsIntroRow(card, r) Then
            If IsCardEndRow(card, r) Then Exit For
            seen = seen + 1
            If seen <= rawCount Then
                expected = introCount + seen
                text = OK1ExcelText(card, r, 2)
                Set matches = rx.Execute(text)
                If matches.count = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2757, , "Ó ïóíêòà â B" & CStr(r) & " îòñóòñòâóåò íîìåð."
                If CLng(matches(0).SubMatches(0)) <> expected Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2758, , _
                    "Íîìåð ïóíêòà B" & CStr(r) & " íå ñîâïàäàåò ñ íîìåðîì áàëëîíà. Ïîâòîðèòå ýêñïîðò ïîñëå ïåðåíóìåðàöèè."
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If seen < rawCount Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2759, , "Â êàðòå ìåíüøå ïóíêòîâ, ÷åì ýêñïîðòèðîâàííûõ ïîçèöèé."
End Sub



Private Function KC14_ProtectedText(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim units As Variant, terms As Variant, item As Variant, p As Long, n As Long
    text = Replace(Trim$(text), Chr(160), " ")
    units = Array("ìì", "ñì", "ì", "øò", "øò.", "êã", "ã", "°", "ãðàä", "ãðàä.")
    For Each item In units
        p = 1
        Do
            p = InStr(p, text, " " & CStr(item), vbTextCompare)
            If p <= 1 Then Exit Do
            If IsNumeric(Mid$(text, p - 1, 1)) Then text = Left$(text, p - 1) & Chr(1) & Mid$(text, p + 1)
            p = p + Len(CStr(item)) + 1
        Loop
    Next item
    terms = Array("ðàçðåç", "ñå÷åíèå", "âèä", "ýñêèç", "äåò.", "ïîç.")
    For Each item In terms
        p = 1
        Do
            p = InStr(p, text, CStr(item) & " ", vbTextCompare)
            If p = 0 Then Exit Do
            n = p + Len(CStr(item))
            text = Left$(text, n - 1) & Chr(1) & Mid$(text, n + 1)
            p = n + 1
        Loop
    Next item
    text = Replace(Replace(text, vbCrLf, vbLf), vbCr, vbLf)
    Do While InStr(text, "  ") > 0: text = Replace(text, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC14_ProtectedText = text
End Function

Private Function KC14_Fit(ByVal rest As String, ByVal col As Long, ByVal probe As Object) As Long
    Dim ends() As Long, count As Long, p As Long, lo As Long, hi As Long, midIndex As Long, best As Long, space As Long
    If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, Replace(rest, Chr(1), " ")) Then KC14_Fit = Len(rest): Exit Function
    ReDim ends(1 To Len(rest) + 1)
    For p = 1 To Len(rest)
        If Mid$(rest, p, 1) = " " Then count = count + 1: ends(count) = p - 1
    Next p
    count = count + 1: ends(count) = Len(rest)
    lo = 1: hi = count
    Do While lo <= hi
        midIndex = (lo + hi) \ 2
        If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, Replace(Left$(rest, ends(midIndex)), Chr(1), " ")) Then
            best = ends(midIndex): lo = midIndex + 1
        Else
            hi = midIndex - 1
        End If
    Loop
    If best > 0 Then
        space = InStrRev(Left$(rest, best), " ")
        If space > 0 Then
            If IsPreposition(Mid$(rest, space + 1, best - space)) Then best = space - 1
        End If
    Else
        ' Òîëüêî íåäåëèìûé òîêåí äëèííåå ÿ÷åéêè: äåëèì ñèìâîëû, íå òåðÿÿ îñòàòîê.
        lo = 1: hi = ends(1)
        Do While lo <= hi
            midIndex = (lo + hi) \ 2
            If ProbeCellFits(probe, col, Replace(Left$(rest, midIndex), Chr(1), " ")) Then best = midIndex: lo = midIndex + 1 Else hi = midIndex - 1
        Loop
    End If
    If best = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3040, , "Â ÿ÷åéêó íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ îäèí ñèìâîë. Ïðîâåðüòå øðèôò è ïîëÿ."
    KC14_Fit = best
End Function

Private Sub KC14_AddVisual(ByRef rows() As VisRow, ByRef count As Long, ByVal group As Long, _
    ByVal a As String, ByVal b As String, ByVal c As String, ByVal d As String, Optional ByVal separator As Boolean = False)
    count = count + 1
    If count > UBound(rows) Then ReDim Preserve rows(1 To count + 50)
    rows(count).col0 = a: rows(count).col1 = b: rows(count).Col2 = c: rows(count).Col3 = d
    rows(count).GrpIdx = group: rows(count).IsSep = separator
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_RenderSegment(ByRef a() As String, ByRef b() As String, ByRef c() As String, ByRef d() As String, _
    ByVal first As Long, ByVal last As Long, ByVal group As Long, ByVal probe As Object, ByRef rows() As VisRow, ByRef count As Long)
    Dim text As String, tools As String, seen As Object, r As Long, value As String, leftLines As Variant, rightLines As Variant
    Dim i As Long, amount As Long, x As String, y As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For r = first To last
        If Len(a(r)) > 0 Then
            If Len(text) > 0 Then text = text & " "
            text = text & a(r)
        End If
        value = Trim$(b(r))
        If Len(value) > 0 Then
            If Not seen.Exists(value) Then
                seen.Add value, True
                If Len(tools) > 0 Then tools = tools & vbLf
                tools = tools & value
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Len(text & tools & c(first) & d(first)) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not ProbeCellFits(probe, 2, c(first)) Or Not ProbeCellFits(probe, 3, d(first)) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3041, , "Îáú¸ì/âðåìÿ íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ â ñòðîêó ïóíêòà " & CStr(group)
    leftLines = WrapTextForTable(text, MAX_CHARS_COL0, 0, probe)
    rightLines = WrapTextForTable(tools, MAX_CHARS_COL1, 1, probe)
    amount = UBound(leftLines)
    If UBound(rightLines) > amount Then amount = UBound(rightLines)
    For i = 1 To amount
        x = "": y = ""
        If i <= UBound(leftLines) Then x = leftLines(i)
        If i <= UBound(rightLines) Then y = rightLines(i)
        If i = 1 Then
            KC14_AddVisual rows, count, group, x, y, c(first), d(first)
        Else
            KC14_AddVisual rows, count, group, x, y, "", ""
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_RenderGroup(ByRef a() As String, ByRef b() As String, ByRef c() As String, ByRef d() As String, _
    ByVal first As Long, ByVal last As Long, ByVal group As Long, ByVal probe As Object, ByRef rows() As VisRow, ByRef count As Long)
    Dim segment As Long, r As Long
    segment = first
    ' Äîïîëíèòåëüíûå îáú¸ì/âðåìÿ â ïðîäîëæåíèè ÿâëÿþòñÿ îòäåëüíîé ïðèâÿçêîé, íå âûáðàñûâàþòñÿ.
    For r = first + 1 To last
        If Len(c(r) & d(r)) > 0 Then
            KC14_RenderSegment a, b, c, d, segment, r - 1, group, probe, rows, count
            segment = r
        End If
    Next r
    KC14_RenderSegment a, b, c, d, segment, last, group, probe, rows, count
End Sub



Private Sub KC14_FillBook(ByVal path As String, ByRef xl As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByRef owns As Boolean)
    Dim candidate As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each candidate In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(candidate.FullName, path, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = candidate: Exit For
        Next candidate
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        xl.Visible = True: xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, True)
        owns = True
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_CheckReady(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As String)
    Dim meta As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set meta = book.Worksheets("ÎÊ_Ñëóæåáíîå")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If meta Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3050, , "Íóæíà ðàáî÷àÿ êíèãà .xlsm v14."
    If StrComp(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3051, , "Êíèãà îòíîñèòñÿ ê äðóãîìó ÷åðòåæó."
    If CStr(meta.Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3052, , "Êàðòà åù¸ íå îáíîâëåíà. Ñíà÷àëà âûïîëíèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà."
    If Not book.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3053, , "Ñíà÷àëà ñîõðàíèòå ðàáî÷óþ êíèãó Excel. Íåñîõðàí¸ííûå ïðàâêè íå èñïîëüçóþòñÿ ïðè çàïîëíåíèè ÎÊ."
End Sub

Private Function KC14_DeletePreview(ByVal draw As Object) As String
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, result As String
    names = draw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If IsReplaceableOKSheet(CStr(names(i))) Then
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & ", "
            result = result & CStr(names(i))
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(result) = 0 Then result = "íåò"
    If Len(result) > 2200 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3054, , "Ñëèøêîì ìíîãî ëèñòîâ äëÿ ïîëíîãî ïîäòâåðæäåíèÿ óäàëåíèÿ."
    KC14_DeletePreview = result
End Function

' Îáùèå ïóòè v14. Ôîðìàò ïðèâÿçêè v12 ñîõðàí¸í äëÿ ñóùåñòâóþùèõ êíèã.
Private Function KC14_Key(ByVal drawing As String) As String
    Dim h As Double, i As Long, n As Long
    h = 5381
    For i = 1 To Len(LCase$(drawing))
        n = AscW(Mid$(LCase$(drawing), i, 1))
        If n < 0 Then n = n + 65536
        h = h * 33# + n
        h = h - Fix(h / 2147483629#) * 2147483629#
    Next i
    KC14_Key = "D" & Hex$(CLng(h))
End Function

Private Function KC14_Setting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal name As String, ByVal fallback As String) As String
    If StrComp(GetSetting("OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), "Owner", ""), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
        KC14_Setting = fallback
    Else
        KC14_Setting = GetSetting("OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), name, fallback)
    End If
End Function

Private Sub KC14_SaveSetting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal name As String, ByVal value As String)
    SaveSetting "OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), "Owner", drawing
    SaveSetting "OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), name, value
End Sub

Private Function KC14_DefaultPath(ByVal drawing As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = KC14_Setting(drawing, "Folder", fso.GetParentFolderName(drawing))
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3000, , "Íåäîñòóïíà ðàáî÷àÿ ïàïêà: " & folder
    KC14_DefaultPath = fso.BuildPath(folder, fso.GetBaseName(drawing) & " — êàðòà êîíòðîëÿ.xlsm")
End Function

Private Function KC14_Resolve(ByVal drawing As String, Optional ByVal required As Boolean = True) As String
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, signature As String, owner As String, path As String, sidecar As String, detail As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Len(drawing) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3001, , "Ñíà÷àëà ñîõðàíèòå ÷åðò¸æ."
    sidecar = drawing & ".okintro-v12.txt"
    On Error GoTo Invalid
    If fso.FileExists(sidecar) Then
        Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(sidecar, 1, False, -1)
        signature = stream.ReadLine: owner = stream.ReadLine: path = stream.ReadLine
        stream.Close: Set stream = Nothing
        If signature <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Or StrComp(owner, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, , "Ïîâðåæäåíà ïðèâÿçêà èëè óêàçàí äðóãîé ÷åðò¸æ. Äëÿ íîâîé êîïèè íå êîïèðóéòå ÷óæîé .okintro-v12.txt; ñîõðàíèòå åãî îòäåëüíî è ïîâòîðèòå çàïóñê áåç íåãî."
        If LCase$(Right$(path, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, , "Â ïðèâÿçêå äîëæíà áûòü .xlsm."
    Else
        path = KC14_DefaultPath(drawing)
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If fso.FileExists(path) Then
        KC14_Resolve = path
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC14_Resolve = KC30_RebindMissing(drawing, path, required)
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, "KC14_Resolve", detail
End Function

Private Function KC14_Service(ByVal path As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), ".ok-service")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then fso.CreateFolder folder
    KC14_Service = folder
End Function

Private Sub KC14_Log(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal text As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, path As String
    If Not KC28_LogOn("Logs.Journal") Then Exit Sub
    On Error Resume Next
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    path = fso.BuildPath(KC14_Service(drawing), "Æóðíàë_ÎÊ.txt")
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Then
        Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(path, True, True)
        stream.Close
    End If
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(path, 8, False, -1)
    stream.WriteLine Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & " | " & text
    stream.Close
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub


Private Function NormalizePartDesignation(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim re As Object
    value = Trim$(Replace(value, ChrW(160), " "))
    value = Replace(value, ChrW(&H2010), "-")
    value = Replace(value, ChrW(&H2011), "-")
    value = Replace(value, ChrW(&H2013), "-")
    value = Replace(value, ChrW(&H2014), "-")
    value = Replace(value, ChrW(&H2212), "-")
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = True
    re.Pattern = "\s*([.\-])\s*"
    value = re.Replace(value, "$1")
    re.Pattern = "^([A-Za-zÀ-ßà-ÿ¨¸0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+|[0-9]+)(-[0-9]+)?$"
    If re.Test(value) Then NormalizePartDesignation = value
End Function


Private Sub EnsureMacroSheetFormat(ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal templatePath As String)
    Dim result As Long
    If Len(templatePath) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3080, , "Íå çàäàí ïóòü ôîðìàòà ëèñòà."
    If StrComp(sheet.GetTemplateName, templatePath, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    sheet.SetTemplateName templatePath
    ' Òîëüêî ïðè ðåàëüíî äðóãîì ôîðìàòå: íå ñîõðàíÿåì èçìåí¸ííûå çàìåòêè
    ' ñòàðîãî ôîðìàòà ïîâåðõ çàìåòîê íîâîãî. Îáû÷íûå çàìåòêè ëèñòà íå óäàëÿåì.
    result = sheet.ReloadTemplate(False)
    If result <> swReloadTemplate_Success Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3081, , _
        "Íå çàãðóæåí ôîðìàò ëèñòà. ReloadTemplate âåðíóë " & CStr(result) & "."
    If StrComp(sheet.GetTemplateName, templatePath, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3082, , "Ïîñëå çàãðóçêè ïóòü ôîðìàòà ëèñòà íå ñîâïàäàåò ñ çàäàííûì."
End Sub


Private Function KC15_IntroLast(ByVal ws As Object) As Long
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object, r As Long, c As Long, last As Long, key As String
    Dim tailStart As Long, hasRegular As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names("OK_IntroArea")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then
        If nm.RefersTo = "=0" Then KC15_IntroLast = 6: Exit Function
        On Error GoTo Invalid
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then GoTo Invalid
        If area.Areas.count <> 1 Or area.row <> 7 Or area.Column <> 1 Or area.Columns.count <> 5 Then GoTo Invalid
        last = area.row + area.rows.count - 1
        If last > 100000 Then GoTo Invalid
        KC15_IntroLast = last
        Exit Function
    End If
    ' Áåçîïàñíàÿ ìèãðàöèÿ ñòàðîé êíèãè: òîëüêî áóêâàëüíûå ÎÊ1-... äî ïåðâîãî ïàðàìåòðà.
    last = 6
    For c = 1 To 5
        r = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, c).End(-4162).row
        If r > last Then last = r
    Next c
    KC15_IntroLast = 6
    For r = 7 To last
        If IsError(ws.cells(r, 1).Value2) Then GoTo Invalid
        key = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 1).Value2))
        If Len(key) > 0 Then
            If StrComp(Left$(key, 4), "ÎÊ1-", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                hasRegular = True
                Exit For
            End If
            tailStart = r
        End If
        KC15_IntroLast = r
    Next r
    If Not hasRegular And tailStart > 0 Then
        If KC15_IntroLast < tailStart + 3 Then KC15_IntroLast = tailStart + 3
    End If
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3150, "KC15_IntroLast", "Ïîâðåæä¸í äèàïàçîí ââîäíûõ OK_IntroArea. Íå ìåíÿéòå åãî âðó÷íóþ; âîññòàíîâèòå êíèãó èç ðåçåðâíîé êîïèè."
End Function


Private Function KC15_IsIntroRow(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long) As Boolean
    KC15_IsIntroRow = (row >= 7 And row <= KC15_IntroLast(ws))
End Function


Private Sub KC15_SetIntroArea(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal last As Long)
    Dim refers As String
    If last < 6 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3151, , "Íåâåðíàÿ ãðàíèöà ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ."
    refers = "=0"
    If last >= 7 Then refers = "='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!$A$7:$E$" & CStr(last)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names("OK_IntroArea").Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:="OK_IntroArea", RefersTo:=refers
End Sub


Private Function KC15_PercentText(ByVal value As Variant) As String
    Dim text As String, n As Double, explicitPercent As Boolean
    If IsError(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3152, , "Îøèáêà Excel â ñòîëáöå Îáú¸ì è ÏÊ."
    text = Trim$(Replace(CStr(value), ChrW(160), " "))
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    explicitPercent = (InStr(text, "%") > 0)
    text = Replace(Replace(text, "%", ""), " ", "")
    If IsNumeric(text) Then
        n = CDbl(text)
        If Not explicitPercent And n >= 0 And n <= 1 Then n = n * 100#
        KC15_PercentText = CStr(n) & "%"
    Else
        KC15_PercentText = Trim$(CStr(value))
    End If
End Function


Private Sub KC15_ReadVariants(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef enabled As Boolean, ByRef text As String)
    Dim flag As String
    flag = UCase$(KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16")))
    enabled = (flag = "TRUE" Or flag = "ÈÑÒÈÍÀ" Or flag = "1" Or flag = "ÄÀ")
    If Not enabled And flag <> "" And flag <> "FALSE" And flag <> "ËÎÆÜ" And flag <> "0" And flag <> "ÍÅÒ" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3155, , "Íåèçâåñòíîå çíà÷åíèå ôëàæêà èñïîëíåíèé."
    text = KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_VariantText", "I17"))
    If enabled And Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3154, , "Âêëþ÷åíà òàáëèöà èñïîëíåíèé: çàïîëíèòå òåêñò â íîâîé ïàíåëè (I12)."
End Sub

Private Function KC15_OwnVariantTables(ByVal model As Object) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, view As Object, tables As Variant, i As Long, tbl As Object
    Dim seen As Object, key As String, firstTable As Long, lastTable As Long
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set view = model.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        tables = view.GetTableAnnotations
        If KC15_TryArrayBounds(tables, firstTable, lastTable) Then
            For i = firstTable To lastTable
                Set tbl = tables(i)
                If ReadFrameTableKey(tbl) = "MF_OK_VARIANT_V15" Then
                    key = FrameTableFeatureName(tbl)
                    If Not seen.Exists(key) Then
                        seen.Add key, True
                        result.Add tbl
                    End If
                End If
            Next i
        End If
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
    Set KC15_OwnVariantTables = result
End Function


Private Function KC15_NewVariantTable(ByVal model As Object, ByVal text As String, ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double) As Object
    Dim tbl As Object, fmt As Object, ann As Object, errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    Dim VARIANT_FONT_POINTS As Long
    VARIANT_FONT_POINTS = CLng(KC28_N("Variant.FontPoints"))
    Dim actualPoints As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set tbl = model.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation(False, x, y, 1, "", 1, 1)
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3161, , "Íå ñîçäàíà òàáëèöà èñïîëíåíèé."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, 0.0494, 0
    ' Ñíà÷àëà òåêñò, çàòåì ëîêàëüíûé ôîðìàò òàáëèöû è ÿ÷åéêè.
    tbl.Text2(0, 0, False) = text
    Set fmt = tbl.GetTextFormat
    If fmt Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3162, , "Íåò ôîðìàòà òåêñòà òàáëèöû èñïîëíåíèé."
    ' Ðàçìåð çàäà¸ì íåïîñðåäñòâåííî â ïóíêòàõ, áåç ïåðåñ÷¸òà ÷åðåç CharHeight.
    ' Èìÿ øðèôòà, íà÷åðòàíèå è íàêëîí ñîõðàíÿþòñÿ.
    fmt.CharHeightInPts = VARIANT_FONT_POINTS
    If Not tbl.SetTextFormat(False, fmt) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3163, , "Íå çàäàí øðèôò òàáëèöû èñïîëíåíèé."
    Set fmt = tbl.GetCellTextFormat(0, 0)
    If fmt Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3164, , "Íåò ôîðìàòà ÿ÷åéêè èñïîëíåíèé."
    fmt.CharHeightInPts = VARIANT_FONT_POINTS
    If Not tbl.SetCellTextFormat(0, 0, False, fmt) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3164, , "Íå çàäàí ôîðìàò ÿ÷åéêè èñïîëíåíèé."
    tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 0) = 2
    tbl.CellTextVerticalJustification(0, 0) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
    tbl.SetRowHeight 0, 0.01275, 0
    ' Ñíà÷àëà íàñòðîéêà ñåòêè; íàðóæíàÿ ãðàíèöà íàçíà÷àåòñÿ ïîñëåäíåé.
    tbl.GridLineWeight = -1
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3165, , "Íåò àííîòàöèè òàáëèöû èñïîëíåíèé."
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(x, y, 0) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3166, , "Íå çàäàíà ïîçèöèÿ òàáëèöû èñïîëíåíèé."
    If Abs(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) - 0.0494) > 0.00005 Or Abs(tbl.GetRowHeight(0) - 0.01275) > 0.00005 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3167, , "Òåêñò èñïîëíåíèé íå ïîìåùàåòñÿ â òàáëèöó 49,400 x 12,750 ìì øðèôòîì 9. Ñîêðàòèòå òåêñò èñïîëíåíèé â íîâîé ïàíåëè Excel (I12)."
    KC15_SetVariantBorder model, tbl
    Set fmt = tbl.GetCellTextFormat(0, 0)
    If fmt Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3169, , "Íå ïîëó÷åí ôîðìàò ÿ÷åéêè ïîñëå óñòàíîâêè øðèôòà."
    actualPoints = CLng(fmt.CharHeightInPts)
    If actualPoints <> VARIANT_FONT_POINTS Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3169, , _
        "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í ðàçìåð øðèôòà èñïîëíåíèé: çàäàíî 9 ïò, ïðî÷èòàíî " & CStr(actualPoints) & " ïò."
    If FrameTextComparable(CStr(tbl.Text2(0, 0, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(text) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3170, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í òåêñò èñïîëíåíèé."
    Set KC15_NewVariantTable = tbl
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC15_NewVariantTable", detail
End Function


Private Sub KC15_PreflightVariant(ByVal model As Object, ByVal text As String)
    Dim probe As Object
    ' Âðåìåííûé îáúåêò âíå ëèñòà ïðîâåðÿåò âìåñòèìîñòü äî óäàëåíèÿ ïðåæíèõ ÎÊ.
    Set probe = KC15_NewVariantTable(model, text, -0.4, -0.4)
    DeleteFrameDuplicate model, probe
End Sub


Private Sub KC15_UpdateVariant(ByVal model As Object, ByVal enabled As Boolean, ByVal text As String, ByVal sheetName As String)
    Dim previous As Collection, old As Variant, tbl As Object, marked As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set previous = KC15_OwnVariantTables(model)
    If enabled Then
        ' Ñíà÷àëà ñîçäà¸ì/ïðîâåðÿåì/ìàðêèðóåì íîâóþ; ïðåæíèå óäàëÿåì ëèøü ïîñëå óñïåõà.
        Set tbl = KC15_NewVariantTable(model, text, 0.0809, KC18_VariantY(sheetName))
        MarkFrameTable model, tbl, "MF_OK_VARIANT_V15"
        If ReadFrameTableKey(tbl) <> "MF_OK_VARIANT_V15" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3171, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà ìàðêèðîâêà òàáëèöû èñïîëíåíèé."
        marked = True
    End If
    For Each old In previous
        DeleteFrameDuplicate model, old
    Next old
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not marked And Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC15_UpdateVariant", detail
End Sub


Private Function KC15_TryArrayBounds(ByVal values As Variant, ByRef first As Long, ByRef last As Long) As Boolean
    ' API ìîæåò âåðíóòü Empty èëè ïóñòîé SAFEARRAY. IsArray ñàìî ïî ñåáå
    ' íå ãàðàíòèðóåò, ÷òî ó ìàññèâà ìîæíî ïðî÷èòàòü ãðàíèöû.
    On Error GoTo NoItems
    If Not IsArray(values) Then Exit Function
    first = LBound(values)
    last = UBound(values)
    KC15_TryArrayBounds = (last >= first)
    Exit Function
NoItems:
    Err.Clear
    first = 0: last = -1
    KC15_TryArrayBounds = False
End Function


Private Sub KC15_SetVariantBorder(ByVal model As Object, ByVal tbl As Object)
    ' Â ïîêàçàííûõ ïîëüçîâàòåëåì íàñòðîéêàõ 0,35 ìì — ñòàíäàðòíàÿ ñòóïåíü Thick.
    ' swLineWeights_e: swLW_THIN=0, swLW_NORMAL=1, swLW_THICK=2, swLW_CUSTOM=10.
    ' Ôèçè÷åñêàÿ òîëùèíà ñòàíäàðòíûõ ñòóïåíåé çàâèñèò îò íàñòðîåê òîëùèí ëèíèé.
    ' Èõ ãëîáàëüíûå/äîêóìåíòíûå íàñòðîéêè çäåñü ÍÅ èçìåíÿþòñÿ.
    ' https://help.solidworks.com/2021/english/api/swconst/SolidWorks.Interop.swconst~SolidWorks.Interop.swconst.swLineWeights_e.html
    Const THICK_PRESET As Long = 2
    Dim actualMode As Long, modeKnown As Boolean, rebuilt As Boolean
    Dim number As Long, detail As String, modeText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = THICK_PRESET
    actualMode = CLng(tbl.BorderLineWeight): modeKnown = True
    If actualMode <> THICK_PRESET Then
        tbl.BorderLineWeight = THICK_PRESET
        rebuilt = model.ForceRebuild3(False)
        actualMode = CLng(tbl.BorderLineWeight)
    End If
    If actualMode <> THICK_PRESET Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3168, "KC15_SetVariantBorder", _
        "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà ñòàíäàðòíàÿ ñòóïåíü ãðàíèöû swLW_THICK = 2 (0,35 ìì â âàøåì ñïèñêå)."
    ' BorderLineWeightCustom îòíîñèòñÿ ê custom è íå ïðîâåðÿåò âûáðàííóþ ñòàíäàðòíóþ ñòóïåíü.
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    number = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    modeText = "íå ïðî÷èòàí"
    If modeKnown Then modeText = CStr(actualMode)
    Err.Raise number, "KC15_SetVariantBorder", detail & vbCrLf & _
        "BorderLineWeight: " & modeText & " (îæèäàåòñÿ 2 = swLW_THICK)."
End Sub


Private Function KC18_VariantY(ByVal sheetName As String) As Double
    Dim n As String
    n = Trim$(sheetName)
    If StrComp(n, "Òèòóëüíûé ëèñò", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC18_VariantY = 0.19090240064
    ElseIf StrComp(n, "ÎÊ1", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC18_VariantY = 0.19125
    ElseIf KC18_IsActiveCardSheet(n) And StrComp(Left$(n, 2), "ÎÊ", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC18_VariantY = 0.1955
    ElseIf KC18_IsActiveCardSheet(n) Or IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(n) Then
        KC18_VariantY = 0.19725
    Else
        ' Äëÿ îñòàëüíûõ èñõîäíûõ ëèñòîâ ñîõðàíÿåì ïðåæíþþ êîîðäèíàòó.
        KC18_VariantY = 0.19125
    End If
End Function

Private Function KC18_IsActiveCardSheet(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    ' Òîëüêî äåéñòâóþùèå ëèñòû; íèêàêèå _old, _old1, _old_2 èëè ïðîèçâîëüíûå èìåíà.
    re.Pattern = "^(ÎÊ[1-9][0-9]*|ÊÝ[1-9][0-9]*|ÊÝ \(Øâû Ëèñò [1-9][0-9]*\))$"
    KC18_IsActiveCardSheet = re.Test(Trim$(sheetName))
End Function





Private Function KC18_NextArchiveGeneration(ByVal drawing As Object) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, maximum As Double, value As Double
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, suffix As String
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3189, , "Íåò ñïèñêà ëèñòîâ äëÿ âûáîðà íîìåðà àðõèâà."
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^ÊÝ\s*\(Øâû\s+Ëèñò\s+[0-9]+\)(_[0-9]{6})?_old(_?[0-9]+)?$"
    For i = first To last
        Set matches = re.Execute(Trim$(CStr(names(i))))
        If matches.count > 0 Then
            suffix = Replace(CStr(matches(0).SubMatches(1)), "_", "")
            value = 1
            If Len(suffix) > 0 Then value = CDbl(suffix)
            If value > maximum Then maximum = value
        End If
    Next i
    If maximum >= 2147483647# Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3190, , "Äîñòèãíóò ïðåäåë íîìåðà êîìïëåêòà àðõèâà ÊÝ."
    KC18_NextArchiveGeneration = CLng(maximum) + 1
End Function


Private Function KC20_IntroCount(ByVal card As Object) As Long
    Dim last As Long, data As Variant, first As Long, Finish As Long, r As Long, c As Long
    Dim hasData As Boolean, text As String, key As String, count As Long
    last = KC15_IntroLast(card)
    If last < 7 Then Exit Function
    data = card.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).Value2
    ' Ïðîâåðÿåì îøèáêè îäèí ðàç íà ìàññèâå, íå îòäåëüíûìè îáðàùåíèÿìè ê Excel.
    For r = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        For c = 1 To 5
            If IsError(data(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3223, "KC20_IntroCount", _
                "Îøèáêà Excel âî ââîäíûõ, ñòðîêà " & CStr(r + 6) & "."
        Next c
    Next r
    first = 1
    Do While first <= UBound(data, 1)
        Finish = first
        Do While Finish < UBound(data, 1)
            If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(Finish + 1, 1)))) > 0 Then Exit Do
            Finish = Finish + 1
        Loop
        key = Trim$(CStr(data(first, 1)))
        hasData = False
        For r = first To Finish
            For c = 2 To 5
                text = Trim$(CStr(data(r, c)))
                If Len(text) > 0 Then
                    ' Êàê â ïåðâîì ìàêðîñå: îäíî çíà÷åíèå 100% íå äåëàåò ïóñòîé áëîê ïóíêòîì.
                    If c <> 4 Then
                        hasData = True
                    ElseIf KC15_PercentText(data(r, c)) <> "100%" Then
                        hasData = True
                    End If
                End If
            Next c
        Next r
        If hasData Then
            If Len(key) = 0 Or Len(Trim$(CStr(data(first, 2)))) = 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3224, "KC20_IntroCount", _
                    "Ó ââîäíîãî áëîêà íåò ìåòêè â A èëè íà÷àëüíîãî òåêñòà â B" & CStr(first + 6) & ". Èñïðàâüòå ïóíêò è ïîâòîðèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà."
            count = count + 1
        End If
        first = Finish + 1
    Loop
    If count > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3225, "KC20_IntroCount", "Ñëèøêîì ìíîãî ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ (ïðåäåë 1000)."
    KC20_IntroCount = count
End Function

Private Function KC20_CheckIntroSync(ByVal model As Object, ByVal card As Object, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim manager As Object, raw As String, resolved As String, parts As Variant
    Dim count As Long, confirmed As Long, firstBalloon As Long, re As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    count = KC20_IntroCount(card)
    Set manager = model.Extension.CustomPropertyManager("")
    If manager Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3226, , "Íå ïðî÷èòàíî ñëóæåáíîå ñâîéñòâî ÷åðòåæà."
    manager.Get2 "KC_IntroSync_v1", raw, resolved
    If Len(raw) = 0 Then
        reason = "Â ÷åðòåæå íåò ïîäòâåðæäåíèÿ ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ."
        GoTo Reconfirm
    End If
    If raw = "PENDING" Then
        reason = "Ïîñëåäíåå èçìåíåíèå áàëëîíîâ íå ïîäòâåðæäåíî óñïåøíîé ïåðåíóìåðàöèåé è ýêñïîðòîì."
        GoTo Reconfirm
    End If
    parts = Split(raw, "|")
    If UBound(parts) <> 3 Then GoTo InvalidRecord
    If parts(0) <> "READY" Then
        reason = "Ïîñëåäíåå èçìåíåíèå áàëëîíîâ íå ïîäòâåðæäåíî óñïåøíîé ïåðåíóìåðàöèåé è ýêñïîðòîì."
        GoTo Reconfirm
    End If
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "^(0|[1-9][0-9]{0,3})$"
    If Not re.Test(CStr(parts(1))) Or Not re.Test(CStr(parts(2))) Then GoTo InvalidRecord
    confirmed = CLng(parts(1)): firstBalloon = CLng(parts(2))
    If confirmed > 1000 Or firstBalloon <> confirmed + 1 Then GoTo InvalidRecord
    If StrComp(CStr(parts(3)), model.GetPathName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
        reason = "Ïîäòâåðæäåíèå îòíîñèòñÿ ê äðóãîìó ïóòè ÷åðòåæà."
        GoTo Reconfirm
    End If
    If count <> confirmed Then
        reason = "Êîëè÷åñòâî ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ èçìåíèëîñü: â ÷åðòåæå ïîäòâåðæäåíî " & CStr(confirmed) & _
            ", ñåé÷àñ â Excel " & CStr(count) & "." & vbCrLf & _
            "Áàëëîíû äîëæíû íà÷èíàòüñÿ ñ " & CStr(count + 1) & ", ðàíåå ïîäòâåðæäåíî íà÷àëî ñ " & CStr(firstBalloon) & "."
        GoTo Reconfirm
    End If
    KC20_CheckIntroSync = True
    Exit Function
InvalidRecord:
    reason = "Ïîâðåæäåíî ñëóæåáíîå ïîäòâåðæäåíèå ââîäíûõ ïóíêòîâ â ÷åðòåæå."
Reconfirm:
    reason = reason & vbCrLf & "Âûïîëíèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà ñ ýòîé êíèãîé Excel, çàòåì ïîâòîðèòå çàïîëíåíèå ÎÊ." & _
        vbCrLf & "Ëèñòû ÷åðòåæà ýòèì çàïóñêîì íå èçìåíåíû."
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "Íå óäàëîñü ïðîâåðèòü ââîäíûå ïóíêòû: " & Err.description & vbCrLf & _
        "Çàïîëíåíèå ÎÊ îñòàíîâëåíî äî èçìåíåíèÿ ëèñòîâ."
End Function


' Òîëüêî îáû÷íûå ÊÝ è ÿâíî íàçâàííûå ïîëüçîâàòåëåì çàãîòîâêè.
Private Function KC21_IsNewKE(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^ÊÝ íîâûé( [1-9][0-9]*)?$"
    KC21_IsNewKE = re.Test(Trim$(sheetName))
End Function

Private Sub KC21_PlanRegularKE(ByVal drawing As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByRef total As Long, ByRef changed As Long)
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, n As String
    Dim candidates As Object
    Erase oldNames: Erase newNames
    total = 0: changed = 0
    KC37_AssertKE1 drawing
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3240, , "Íå ïîëó÷åí ñïèñîê ëèñòîâ äëÿ óïîðÿäî÷åíèÿ ÊÝ."
    Set candidates = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    candidates.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ReDim oldNames(1 To last - first + 1): ReDim newNames(1 To last - first + 1)
    For i = first To last
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(n) > 1 Or KC21_IsNewKE(n) Then
            total = total + 1
            oldNames(total) = n
            newNames(total) = "ÊÝ" & CStr(total + 1)
            candidates.Add n, True
            If StrComp(n, newNames(total), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then changed = changed + 1
        End If
    Next i
    If total = 0 Then
        Erase oldNames: Erase newNames
        Exit Sub
    End If
    ReDim Preserve oldNames(1 To total): ReDim Preserve newNames(1 To total)
    For i = 1 To total
        If SheetExistsExact(drawing, newNames(i)) And Not candidates.Exists(newNames(i)) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3241, , "Èìÿ " & newNames(i) & " çàíÿòî ïîñòîðîííèì ëèñòîì."
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function KC21_MappedName(ByVal name As String, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim i As Long
    KC21_MappedName = name
    For i = 1 To total
        If StrComp(name, oldNames(i), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            KC21_MappedName = newNames(i)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Private Function KC21_MappedSelection(ByVal value As String, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim items As Variant, i As Long
    KC21_MappedSelection = value
    If Len(value) = 0 Or value = "*" Then Exit Function
    items = Split(value, vbLf)
    For i = LBound(items) To UBound(items)
        items(i) = KC21_MappedName(CStr(items(i)), oldNames, newNames, total)
    Next i
    KC21_MappedSelection = Join(items, vbLf)
End Function

Private Function KC21_FreeTempName(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal prefix As String, ByVal ordinal As Long) As String
    Dim candidate As String, suffix As Long
    candidate = prefix & CStr(ordinal)
    Do While SheetExistsExact(drawing, candidate)
        suffix = suffix + 1
        candidate = prefix & CStr(ordinal) & "_" & CStr(suffix)
    Loop
    KC21_FreeTempName = candidate
End Function

Private Sub KC21_SetSheetName(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal target As String)
    If StrComp(sheet.GetName, target, vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If StrComp(CStr(sheet.GetName), "ÊÝ1", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Or StrComp(target, "ÊÝ1", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Ñìåíà èìåíè ôèêñèðîâàííîãî ÊÝ1 çàïðåùåíà."
    If SheetExistsExact(drawing, target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3242, , "Èìÿ ëèñòà çàíÿòî: " & target
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3254, , _
        "Íå àêòèâèðîâàí ëèñò äëÿ ïåðåèìåíîâàíèÿ: " & CStr(sheet.GetName)
    sheet.SetName target
    If StrComp(sheet.GetName, target, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3243, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíî èìÿ ëèñòà: " & target
    If Not SheetExistsExact(drawing, target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3244, , "Íå íàéäåí ïåðåèìåíîâàííûé ëèñò: " & target
End Sub

Private Sub KC21_RenameRegularKE(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long, _
    ByRef insertAfterSheet As String, ByVal bookPath As String, ByRef reviewRequired As Boolean)
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3711, , "Ñîãëàñîâàíèå ÊÝ ïåðåíåñåíî â ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà. Âòîðîé ìàêðîñ ïåðåèìåíîâàíèå íå âûïîëíÿåò."
End Sub



' Ñðàâíåíèå òîëüêî íîìåðîâ ãðóïï è îáîçíà÷åíèé. Ðàçìåðû è ðèñóíêè íå ÷èòàåì.
Private Function KC22_WeldText(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = Replace(text, vbCrLf, vbLf)
    text = Replace(text, vbCr, vbLf)
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), " ")
    KC22_WeldText = Trim$(text)
End Function

Private Function KC22_WeldHeaders(ByVal table As Object) As Boolean
    Dim g As Long, c As Long
    If table.ColumnCount <> 4 * KE_GROUPS_COUNT Or table.rowCount < 2 Then Exit Function
    For g = 0 To KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1
        c = g * 4
        If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(0, c, False))) <> "¹" Then Exit Function
        If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(0, c + 1, False))) <> "Îáîçíà÷åíèå" Then Exit Function
    Next g
    KC22_WeldHeaders = True
End Function

' 0 = äåéñòâóþùèõ ëèñòîâ íåò; 1 = ñîâïàäàåò; 2 = íîìåðà/îáîçíà÷åíèÿ îòëè÷àþòñÿ;
' 3 = êîìïëåêò íåïîëíûé, òàáëèöà íåîäíîçíà÷íà ëèáî ðåçóëüòàò íå óäàëîñü ïðî÷èòàòü.
Private Function KC22_CompareWeldSheets(ByVal drawing As Object, ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, _
    ByVal count As Long, ByRef reason As String) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, n As String
    Dim active As Object, seen As Object, pages As Long, page As Long, sheetName As String
    Dim originalSheet As String, view As Object, tables As Variant, tFirst As Long, tLast As Long
    Dim table As Object, found As Object, matches As Long, featureName As String
    Dim perPage As Long, pageCount As Long, rowsData As Long, idx As Long, row As Long, g As Long, col As Long
    Dim expectedNumber As String, expectedDesignation As String, result As Long, different As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    result = 3
    reason = ""
    originalSheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    If count <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3270, , "Íåò ñãðóïïèðîâàííûõ øâîâ äëÿ ïðîâåðêè."
    perPage = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    pages = (count + perPage - 1) \ perPage
    Set active = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    active.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3271, , "Íå ïðî÷èòàí ñïèñîê ëèñòîâ øâîâ."
    For i = first To last
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If IsActiveKEWeldSheet(n) Then active.Add n, n
    Next i
    If active.count = 0 Then
        result = 0
        GoTo Finish
    End If
    If active.count <> pages Then
        reason = "×èñëî äåéñòâóþùèõ ëèñòîâ øâîâ: " & CStr(active.count) & "; ïî òåêóùèì äàííûì òðåáóåòñÿ " & CStr(pages) & "."
        GoTo Finish
    End If
    For page = 1 To pages
        sheetName = "ÊÝ (Øâû Ëèñò " & CStr(page) & ")"
        If Not active.Exists(sheetName) Then
            reason = "Íå íàéäåí äåéñòâóþùèé ëèñò " & sheetName & "."
            GoTo Finish
        End If
    Next page
    ' Íîìåð ñòðàíèöû îïðåäåëÿåò ñîîòâåòñòâèå äàííûì, ïîðÿäîê âêëàäîê äëÿ ñðàâíåíèÿ íå âàæåí.
    For page = 1 To pages
        sheetName = CStr(active("ÊÝ (Øâû Ëèñò " & CStr(page) & ")"))
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3272, , "Íå àêòèâèðîâàí " & sheetName & "."
        idx = (page - 1) * perPage + 1
        pageCount = count - idx + 1
        If pageCount > perPage Then pageCount = perPage
        rowsData = (pageCount + KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ KE_GROUPS_COUNT
        Set found = Nothing
        matches = 0
        Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
        Set view = drawing.GetFirstView
        Do While Not view Is Nothing
            tables = view.GetTableAnnotations
            If KC15_TryArrayBounds(tables, tFirst, tLast) Then
                For i = tFirst To tLast
                    Set table = tables(i)
                    If KC22_WeldHeaders(table) Then
                        featureName = FrameTableFeatureName(table)
                        If Len(featureName) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3273, , "Íå îïðåäåëåíà òàáëèöà øâîâ íà " & sheetName & "."
                        If Not seen.Exists(featureName) Then
                            seen.Add featureName, True
                            matches = matches + 1
                            Set found = table
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
            Set view = view.GetNextView
        Loop
        If matches <> 1 Then
            reason = "Íà " & sheetName & " íàéäåíî òàáëèö øâîâ: " & CStr(matches) & ". Îæèäàåòñÿ îäíà."
            GoTo Finish
        End If
        If found.rowCount <> rowsData + 1 Then
            reason = "Íà " & sheetName & " èçìåíåíî êîëè÷åñòâî ñòðîê òàáëèöû øâîâ."
            GoTo Finish
        End If
        For g = 0 To KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1
            col = g * 4
            For row = 1 To rowsData
                expectedNumber = "": expectedDesignation = ""
                If idx <= count Then
                    expectedNumber = welds(idx).NumberLabel
                    expectedDesignation = BuildWeldDesignation(welds(idx))
                    idx = idx + 1
                End If
                If KC22_WeldText(CStr(found.Text2(row, col, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(expectedNumber) Then different = True
                If KC22_WeldText(CStr(found.Text2(row, col + 1, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(expectedDesignation) Then different = True
            Next row
        Next g
    Next page
    If different Then
        result = 2
        reason = "Èçìåíèëèñü íîìåðà ãðóïï øâîâ èëè îáîçíà÷åíèÿ."
    Else
        result = 1
    End If
Finish:
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, originalSheet) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3274, , "Íå âîññòàíîâëåí àêòèâíûé ëèñò ïîñëå ïðîâåðêè øâîâ."
    End If
    KC22_CompareWeldSheets = result
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "Íå óäàëîñü ïîäòâåðäèòü êîìïëåêò ÊÝ øâîâ: " & Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
    KC22_CompareWeldSheets = 3
End Function




' Îáùàÿ ðàçìåòêà v16: A:E îñòà¸òñÿ íà ïðåæíèõ ñòðîêàõ; ñïðàâà íîâàÿ ïàíåëü ðåêâèçèòîâ.
Private Function KC23_NewLayout(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim nm As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names("OK_HeaderLayout")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    KC23_NewLayout = (nm.RefersTo = "=""OK_HEADER_V16""")
End Function

Private Function KC23_Field(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal legacyAddress As String) As Object
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object
    If KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        On Error Resume Next
        Set nm = ws.Parent.names(fieldName)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If nm Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3300, , "Íå íàéäåí äèàïàçîí " & fieldName & ". Îáíîâèòå Excel-ìîäóëü è âûïîëíèòå OK_UpdateEditor."
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3301, , "Äèàïàçîí " & fieldName & " îòíîñèòñÿ ê äðóãîìó ëèñòó."
        Set KC23_Field = area
    Else
        Set KC23_Field = ws.Range(legacyAddress)
    End If
End Function

Private Function KC23_CellText(ByVal area As Object) As String
    Dim value As Variant
    value = area.cells(1, 1).Value2
    If IsError(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3302, , "Îøèáêà Excel â " & area.address(False, False) & "."
    KC23_CellText = Trim$(CStr(value))
End Function

Private Function KC23_FixedCell(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal index As Long) As Object
    Dim area As Object
    Set area = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_FixedRows", "I7:I9")
    If area.rows.count <> 3 Or area.Columns.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3303, , "Ïîâðåæä¸í äèàïàçîí OK_FixedRows."
    Set KC23_FixedCell = area.cells(index + 1, 1)
End Function

Private Sub KC23_Name(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal addressText As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names(fieldName).Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:=fieldName, RefersTo:="='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!" & ws.Range(addressText).address
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_RegisterNames(ByVal ws As Object)
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_HeaderRoles", "H4:H8"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_HeaderNames", "I4:I8"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I11"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_VariantText", "I12"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_Mass", "I17"
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå" Then KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "I18"
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_FixedRows", "I20:I22"
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) = "Ðåêâèçèòû èçäåëèÿ" Then
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductData", "L4:L6"
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4"
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5"
        If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
            KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18"
        Else
            KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "L6"
        End If
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names("OK_HeaderLayout").Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:="OK_HeaderLayout", RefersTo:="=""OK_HEADER_V16"""
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_Band(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal addressText As String, ByVal label As String)
    With ws.Range(addressText)
        .Merge
        .cells(1, 1).Value2 = label
        .Font.Bold = True
        .Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
        .Borders.LineStyle = 1
        .WrapText = True
    End With
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_EnsureLayout(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim mass As Variant, fixedRows As Variant, flag As Variant, variantText As Variant, summary As Variant
    Dim roles As Variant, i As Long, box As Object
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) = "Ðåêâèçèòû èçäåëèÿ" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3407, , "Ïîâðåæäåíà ìåòêà ïàíåëè ðåêâèçèòîâ. Âîññòàíîâèòå èìåíîâàííûé äèàïàçîí OK_HeaderLayout èç ðåçåðâíîé êîïèè; äàííûå íå î÷èùàëèñü."
        ' Äî ïåðâîãî Clear ñîõðàíÿåì âñå ñòàðûå ïîëÿ, âêëþ÷àÿ âûêëþ÷åííûé òåêñò èñïîëíåíèé.
        mass = ws.Range("I3").Value2
        fixedRows = ws.Range("I7:I9").Value2
        flag = ws.Range("I16").Value2
        variantText = ws.Range("I17").Value2
        summary = ws.Range("I11:I14").Value2
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("H22:I29")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3304, , "Â H22:I29 åñòü ïîñòîðîííèå äàííûå. Ïåðåíåñèòå èõ äî îáíîâëåíèÿ ïàíåëè ðåêâèçèòîâ."
        On Error Resume Next
        ws.CheckBoxes("okVariants").Delete
        On Error GoTo 0
        ws.Range("H3:I29").UnMerge
        ws.Range("H3:I29").Clear
        KC23_RegisterNames ws
        roles = Array("Ðàçðàá.", "Ïðîâ.", "Ñîãëàñîâàíî", "Íà÷. ËÒÊ", "Í. êîíòð.")
        For i = 0 To 4
            ws.cells(4 + i, 8).Value2 = roles(i)
        Next i
        ws.Range("I17").Value2 = mass
        ws.Range("I20:I22").Value2 = fixedRows
        If IsEmpty(flag) Or Len(CStr(flag)) = 0 Then flag = False
        ws.Range("I11").Value2 = flag
        ws.Range("I12").Value2 = variantText
        ws.Range("I24:I27").Value2 = summary
    End If
    KC29_EnsureHeaderData ws
    ws.Columns("H").ColumnWidth = 26
    ws.Columns("I").ColumnWidth = 75
    KC23_Band ws, "H3:I3", "Øàïêà ÎÊ1 / ÊÝ1 — äîëæíîñòü è ôàìèëèÿ"
    With ws.Range("H4:I8")
        .NumberFormat = "@"
        .Font.name = "GOST type A"
        .Font.Size = 14
        .Font.Italic = True
        .Font.Bold = False
        .Borders.LineStyle = 1
        .VerticalAlignment = -4108
        .WrapText = True
    End With
    ws.Range("I4:I8").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
    For i = 4 To 8
        If ws.rows(i).RowHeight < 22 Then ws.rows(i).RowHeight = 22
    Next i
    KC23_Band ws, "H10:I10", "Èñïîëíåíèÿ — òàáëèöà â ðàìêàõ ÎÊ / ÊÝ"
    ws.Range("I11").NumberFormat = ";;;"
    ws.Range("H12").Value2 = "Òåêñò èñïîëíåíèé"
    With ws.Range("I12:I14")
        .Merge
        .NumberFormat = "@"
        .WrapText = True
        .VerticalAlignment = -4160
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=2
    End With
    With ws.Range("H15:I15")
        .Merge
        .Value2 = "Ôëàæîê âûêëþ÷åí — ñîáñòâåííàÿ òàáëèöà èñïîëíåíèé óäàëÿåòñÿ ïðè çàïîëíåíèè."
        .WrapText = True
        .Font.Size = 9
    End With
    ws.Range("H17").Value2 = "Ìàññà äåòàëè, êã"
    With ws.Range("I17")
        .NumberFormat = "0.###"
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=4, Color:=RGB(31, 63, 94)
    End With
    ws.Range("H19").Value2 = "Ñòðîêà"
    ws.Range("I19").Value2 = "Ïåðâûå òðè ñòðîêè ÎÊ1"
    ws.Range("H19:I19").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("H19:I19").Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
    ws.Range("H19:I22").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("H19:I22").WrapText = True
    ws.Range("H20:I22").NumberFormat = "@"
    For i = 0 To 2: ws.cells(20 + i, 8).Value2 = Format$(i + 1, "00"): Next i
    ws.Range("H24").Value2 = "Çàïîëíåííûõ ïóíêòîâ"
    ws.Range("H25").Value2 = "Ïåðâûé áàëëîí"
    ws.Range("H26").Value2 = "Ïóñòûõ áëîêîâ"
    ws.Range("H27").Value2 = "Íåò íà÷àëüíîãî òåêñòà"
    ws.Range("H24:I27").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("I24:I27").NumberFormat = "0"
    ws.Range("H20:I22").rows.AutoFit
    ws.Range("H29").Value2 = "ÎÊ: åäèíûé ñïèñîê v10"
    ws.Range("H29").Font.Size = 9
    ws.Range("H29").Font.Color = RGB(110, 110, 110)
End Sub

Private Function KC23_ReadHeader(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef roles() As String, ByRef surnames() As String) As Boolean
    Dim roleArea As Object, nameArea As Object, i As Long
    ReDim roles(1 To 5): ReDim surnames(1 To 5)
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then Exit Function
    Set roleArea = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_HeaderRoles", "H4:H8")
    Set nameArea = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_HeaderNames", "I4:I8")
    If roleArea.rows.count <> 5 Or roleArea.Columns.count <> 1 Or nameArea.rows.count <> 5 Or nameArea.Columns.count <> 1 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3305, , "Øàïêà äîëæíà ñîäåðæàòü ïÿòü äîëæíîñòåé è ïÿòü ôàìèëèé."
    For i = 1 To 5
        roles(i) = KC23_CellText(roleArea.cells(i, 1))
        surnames(i) = KC23_CellText(nameArea.cells(i, 1))
    Next i
    KC23_ReadHeader = True
End Function

Private Sub KC23_CopyPanel(ByVal source As Object, ByVal target As Object)
    Dim roles() As String, surnames() As String, haveHeader As Boolean
    Dim flag As Variant, text As Variant, i As Long
    Dim primaryText As String, havePrimary As Boolean
    Dim kd As String, itemName As String, equipment As String, haveProduct As Boolean
    If source Is Nothing Then
        KC23_EnsureLayout target
        Exit Sub
    End If
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(source, primaryText)
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(source, kd, itemName, equipment)
    haveHeader = KC23_ReadHeader(source, roles, surnames)
    flag = KC23_Field(source, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16").Value2
    text = KC23_Field(source, "OK_VariantText", "I17").Value2
    KC23_EnsureLayout target
    If havePrimary Then target.Range("L6").Value2 = primaryText
    If haveProduct Then
        target.Range("L4").Value2 = kd
        target.Range("L5").Value2 = itemName
        target.Range("I18").Value2 = equipment
    End If
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16").Value2 = flag
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantText", "I17").Value2 = text
    If haveHeader Then
        For i = 1 To 5
            target.cells(3 + i, 8).Value2 = roles(i)
            target.cells(3 + i, 9).Value2 = surnames(i)
        Next i
    End If
End Sub


Private Function KC23_PreviousSignTables(ByVal model As Object, ByVal topY As Double) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, seen As Object, view As Object, tables As Variant, tbl As Object
    Dim first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, key As String, featureName As String
    Dim ann As Object, pos As Variant, legacy As Boolean, width As Double
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set view = model.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        tables = view.GetTableAnnotations
        If KC15_TryArrayBounds(tables, first, last) Then
            For i = first To last
                Set tbl = tables(i)
                key = ReadFrameTableKey(tbl)
                legacy = False
                If Len(key) = 0 And tbl.rowCount = 5 And tbl.ColumnCount = 1 Then
                    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
                    If Not ann Is Nothing Then
                        pos = ann.GetPosition
                        If IsArray(pos) Then
                            width = tbl.GetColumnWidth(0)
                            If Abs(CDbl(pos(1)) - topY) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M Or _
                                Abs(CDbl(pos(1)) - 0.176) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M Then
                                legacy = (Abs(CDbl(pos(0)) - 0.0055) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M And Abs(width - 0.0234) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M) Or _
                                    (Abs(CDbl(pos(0)) - 0.0289) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M And Abs(width - 0.0364) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M)
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                If key = "MF_SIGN_ROLES_V16" Or key = "MF_SIGN_NAMES_V16" Or _
                    key = "MF_SIGN_ROLES_V18" Or key = "MF_SIGN_NAMES_V18" Or legacy Then
                    If legacy Then
                        If Not AllowLegacyFrameMigration() Then _
                            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3331, , "Çàìåíà ïðåæíèõ òàáëèö øàïêè íå ðàçðåøåíà. Íîâàÿ øàïêà ïîâåðõ íèõ íå âñòàâëåíà."
                    End If
                    featureName = FrameTableFeatureName(tbl)
                    If Len(featureName) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3332, , "Íå îïðåäåë¸í Feature ïðåæíåé øàïêè."
                    If Not seen.Exists(featureName) Then
                        seen.Add featureName, True
                        result.Add tbl
                    End If
                End If
            Next i
        End If
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
    Set KC23_PreviousSignTables = result
End Function

' Øàïêà — îáû÷íûå ïðèìå÷àíèÿ, áåç îáùèõ òàáëèö è îãðàíè÷åíèÿ âûñîòû ñòðîêè.
' X/Y çàäàþò âåðõíèé ëåâûé óãîë ëîãè÷åñêîé ÿ÷åéêè â ìåòðàõ.
Private Sub KC24_SheetContext(ByVal model As Object)
    model.SetPickMode
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    model.EditSheet
    If Not model.ActivateView("") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3340, , "Íå âêëþ÷¸í êîíòåêñò ëèñòà äëÿ ïðèìå÷àíèé øàïêè."
End Sub

Private Sub KC24_DeleteNote(ByVal model As Object, ByVal note As Object)
    Dim ann As Object
    If note Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3341, , "Íåò àííîòàöèè óäàëÿåìîãî ïðèìå÷àíèÿ."
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    If Not ann.Select3(False, Nothing) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3342, , "Íå âûáðàíî ñëóæåáíîå ïðèìå÷àíèå: " & CStr(note.GetName)
    If model.SelectionManager.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1) <> 1 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3343, , "Äëÿ óäàëåíèÿ ïðèìå÷àíèÿ âûäåëåí íå îäèí îáúåêò."
    If Not model.Extension.DeleteSelection2(0) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3344, , "Íå óäàëåíî ñëóæåáíîå ïðèìå÷àíèå øàïêè."
    model.ClearSelection2 True
End Sub

Private Function KC24_OwnedSignNotes(ByVal model As Object) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, view As Object, notes As Variant, note As Object
    Dim lo As Long, hi As Long, i As Long, nm As String
    Set view = model.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        notes = view.GetNotes
        If KC15_TryArrayBounds(notes, lo, hi) Then
            For i = lo To hi
                Set note = notes(i)
                nm = CStr(note.GetName)
                If Left$(nm, Len("MF_SIGN17_")) = "MF_SIGN17_" Then result.Add note
            Next i
        End If
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
    Set KC24_OwnedSignNotes = result
End Function



Private Function KC25_HeaderTemplatePath() As String
    Dim app As Object, fso As Object, macroPath As String, candidate As String
    Set app = Application.SldWorks
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Len(gKC25TemplatePath) > 0 Then
        If fso.FileExists(gKC25TemplatePath) Then
            KC25_HeaderTemplatePath = gKC25TemplatePath
            Exit Function
        End If
        gKC25TemplatePath = ""
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    macroPath = CStr(app.GetCurrentMacroPathName)
    On Error GoTo 0
    candidate = KC28_S("Sign.Template")
