    If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then candidate = ParentFolderOfFile(macroPath) & KC28_S("Sign.Template")
    If fso.FileExists(candidate) Then gKC25TemplatePath = candidate
    If Len(gKC25TemplatePath) = 0 Then
        candidate = BrowseForFile(app, "Øàáëîíû òàáëèö SOLIDWORKS (*.sldtbt),*.sldtbt", _
            "Âûáåðèòå øàáëîí øàïêè ñ íóëåâûì âåðòèêàëüíûì îòñòóïîì", ParentFolderOfFile(macroPath))
        If Len(candidate) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3360, , "Øàáëîí øàïêè íå âûáðàí."
        If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3361, , "Øàáëîí íå íàéäåí: " & candidate
        If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(candidate)) <> "sldtbt" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3362, , "Âûáåðèòå ôàéë .sldtbt."
        gKC25TemplatePath = candidate
    End If
    KC25_HeaderTemplatePath = gKC25TemplatePath
End Function

Private Function KC25_NewSignTable(ByVal model As Object, ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double, _
    ByVal width As Double, ByRef values() As String, ByVal forSurnames As Boolean) As Object
    Dim tbl As Object, ann As Object, fmt As Object, i As Long, ok As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    Dim actualHeight As Double, actualGap As Double
    Dim templatePath As String, columnToDelete As Long
    Dim rowHeightM As Double
    rowHeightM = KC28_N("Sign.RowHeight")
    Const sizeCanChange As Long = 0 ' swTableRowColChange_TableSizeCanChange
    On Error GoTo Failed
    templatePath = KC25_HeaderTemplatePath()
    ' Ïðè íåïóñòîì ïóòè SW èãíîðèðóåò Rows/Columns è áåð¸ò ñòðóêòóðó èç øàáëîíà.
    Set tbl = model.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation(False, x, y, 1, templatePath, 5, 1)
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3320, , "Íå ñîçäàíà òàáëèöà øàïêè 1 x 5."
    If tbl.rowCount <> 5 Or (tbl.ColumnCount <> 1 And tbl.ColumnCount <> 2) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3363, , "Â øàáëîíå íóæíû 5 ñòðîê è 1 ëèáî 2 êîëîíêè. Ïîëó÷åíî: " & _
            CStr(tbl.rowCount) & " ñòðîê, " & CStr(tbl.ColumnCount) & " êîëîíîê."
    ' Èç 5 x 2 îñòàâëÿåì íóæíóþ êîëîíêó, ñîõðàíÿÿ èìåííî å¸ îôîðìëåíèå.
    If tbl.ColumnCount = 2 Then
        If forSurnames Then columnToDelete = 0 Else columnToDelete = 1
        If Not tbl.DeleteColumn2(columnToDelete, True) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3364, , "Íå âûäåëåíà íóæíàÿ êîëîíêà øàáëîíà."
    End If
    If tbl.ColumnCount <> 1 Or tbl.rowCount <> 5 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3365, , "Íå ïîëó÷åíà òàáëèöà 1 x 5."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    tbl.anchorType = 1 ' swBOMConfigurationAnchor_TopLeft
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, width, 0
    For i = 0 To 4: tbl.Text2(i, 0, False) = values(i + 1): Next i
    ' Íå ïåðåóñòàíàâëèâàåì øðèôò è âíóòðåííèå îòñòóïû: èñïîëüçóåì îôîðìëåíèå .sldtbt.
    For i = 0 To 4
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(i, 0) = 1
        tbl.CellTextVerticalJustification(i, 0) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
    Next i
    For i = 0 To 4
        actualHeight = tbl.SetRowHeight(i, rowHeightM, sizeCanChange)
    Next i
    ' Ðàçëèíîâêà óæå åñòü â ôîðìàòå ëèñòà; âòîðóþ ñåòêó ïîâåðõ íå¸ íå ðèñóåì.
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = -1
    tbl.GridLineWeight = -1
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3324, , "Íåò àííîòàöèè øàïêè."
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(x, y, 0#) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3325, , "Íå çàäàíà ïîçèöèÿ øàïêè."
    If Abs(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) - width) > 0.00005 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3326, , "Øèðèíà òàáëèöû øàïêè îòëè÷àåòñÿ îò çàäàííîé."
    For i = 0 To 4
        If FrameTextComparable(CStr(tbl.Text2(i, 0, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(values(i + 1)) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3327, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í òåêñò ñòðîêè øàïêè."
        actualHeight = tbl.GetRowHeight(i)
        actualGap = tbl.GetRowVerticalGap(i)
        Set fmt = tbl.GetCellTextFormat(i, 0)
        If Abs(actualHeight - rowHeightM) > 0.00005 Then
            detail = "SW íå óñòàíîâèë çàäàííóþ âûñîòó ñòðîêè øàáëîíà øàïêè: " & CStr(rowHeightM * 1000#) & " ìì." & vbCrLf & _
                "Ñòðîêà: " & CStr(i + 1) & "; òåêñò: " & values(i + 1) & vbCrLf & _
                "Øèðèíà: " & Format$(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) * 1000#, "0.000") & " ìì" & vbCrLf & _
                "Ôàêòè÷åñêàÿ âûñîòà: " & Format$(actualHeight * 1000#, "0.000") & " ìì" & vbCrLf & _
                "Âåðòèêàëüíûé îòñòóï: " & Format$(actualGap * 1000#, "0.000") & " ìì" & vbCrLf & _
                "Øðèôò: " & CStr(fmt.TypeFaceName) & "; ðàçìåð: " & CStr(fmt.CharHeightInPts) & " ïò." & vbCrLf & _
                "Øðèôò è îòñòóïû íå ïåðåçàïèñûâàëèñü. Øàáëîí: " & templatePath
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3328, , detail
        End If
        If CLng(fmt.CharHeightInPts) <> CLng(KC28_N("Sign.FontPoints")) Or Not fmt.Italic Or fmt.Bold Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3329, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í øðèôò øàïêè èç Sign.FontPoints: " & KC28_S("Sign.FontPoints") & " ïò; òðåáóåòñÿ íàêëîííûé, íå ïîëóæèðíûé."
        If StrComp(CStr(fmt.TypeFaceName), KC28_S("Sign.FontName"), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3330, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæä¸í øðèôò " & KC28_S("Sign.FontName") & ". Ïðîâåðüòå øàáëîí è óñòàíîâêó øðèôòà."
    Next i
    Set KC25_NewSignTable = tbl
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC25_NewSignTable", detail
End Function

Private Sub KC23_PreflightHeader(ByVal model As Object)
    Dim tbl As Object
    KC24_SheetContext model
    Set tbl = KC25_NewSignTable(model, -0.4, -0.4, KC28_N("Sign.RolesWidth"), gKC23Roles, False)
    DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    Set tbl = KC25_NewSignTable(model, -0.4, -0.4, KC28_N("Sign.NamesWidth"), gKC23Surnames, True)
    DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_UpdateSignHeader(ByVal model As Object, ByVal topY As Double)
    Dim previousTables As Collection, previousNotes As Collection, item As Variant
    Dim rolesTable As Object, namesTable As Object, ready As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    If Not gKC23HeaderReady Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    KC24_SheetContext model
    Set previousTables = KC23_PreviousSignTables(model, topY)
    Set previousNotes = KC24_OwnedSignNotes(model)
    Set rolesTable = KC25_NewSignTable(model, KC28_N("Sign.RolesX"), topY, KC28_N("Sign.RolesWidth"), gKC23Roles, False)
    MarkFrameTable model, rolesTable, "MF_SIGN_ROLES_V18"
    Set namesTable = KC25_NewSignTable(model, KC28_N("Sign.NamesX"), topY, KC28_N("Sign.NamesWidth"), gKC23Surnames, True)
    MarkFrameTable model, namesTable, "MF_SIGN_NAMES_V18"
    ready = True
    ' Ñòàðóþ øàïêó óäàëÿåì òîëüêî ïîñëå ïðîâåðêè îáåèõ íîâûõ òàáëèö.
    For Each item In previousNotes: KC24_DeleteNote model, item: Next item
    For Each item In previousTables: DeleteFrameDuplicate model, item: Next item
    model.SetSaveFlag
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not ready Then
        If Not rolesTable Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, rolesTable
        If Not namesTable Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, namesTable
    End If
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC23_UpdateSignHeader", detail
End Sub


' Øàáëîíû, à íå ðàññ÷èòàííûå ðàçìåðû. ×èñëà è äîïóñêè çàïîëíÿþòñÿ ïî ÊÄ.
' Èíäåêñû çàïèñàíû îáû÷íûìè ñèìâîëàìè: e1, g1, K1.
' Èñòî÷íèêè: ïðèñëàííûå ÃÎÑÒ 23518-79, òàáë. 2–20;
' ÃÎÑÒ 14771-76, òàáë. 2–47. Ïàðàìåòðû ïîäãîòîâêè êðîìîê ñþäà íå âêëþ÷àþòñÿ.
Private Function KC26_WeldTypeKey(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim text As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    ' Ëàòèíñêèå âèçóàëüíî ïîõîæèå áóêâû ïðèâîäèì ê îáîçíà÷åíèÿì ÃÎÑÒ.
    text = Replace(text, "C", "Ñ")
    text = Replace(text, "T", "Ò")
    text = Replace(text, "H", "Í")
    text = Replace(text, "Y", "Ó")
    KC26_WeldTypeKey = text
End Function

Private Function KC26_SizeTemplate(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    Dim joint As String, key As String
    Dim standard14771 As Boolean, standard23518 As Boolean
    joint = KC26_WeldTypeKey(w.weldType)
    standard14771 = (InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "14771", vbTextCompare) > 0)
    standard23518 = (InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "23518", vbTextCompare) > 0)
    If standard14771 = standard23518 Then Exit Function
    If standard23518 Then key = "23518|" & joint Else key = "14771|" & joint
    If gKC28Sizes Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3489, , "Øàáëîíû ðàçìåðîâ íå çàãðóæåíû."
    If gKC28Sizes.Exists(key) Then KC26_SizeTemplate = CStr(gKC28Sizes(key))
End Function


' v17: ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå õðàíèòñÿ òîëüêî â ïàíåëè Excel, íå â èìåíè ôàéëà.







' v18: ðåêâèçèòû èçäåëèÿ â Excel; èìÿ ôàéëà áîëüøå íå èñòî÷íèê øàïêè.







' Åäèíûé ñíèìîê íàñòðîåê íà çàïóñê. Íåèçâåñòíûå êëþ÷è è ïðîïóñêè — îøèáêà.
Private Function KC28_S(ByVal key As String) As String
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3450, , "Íàñòðîéêè íå çàãðóæåíû."
    If Not gKC28.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3451, , "Íåò íàñòðîéêè " & key
    KC28_S = CStr(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_N(ByVal key As String) As Double
    KC28_N = CDbl(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_B(ByVal key As String) As Boolean
    KC28_B = (KC28_N(key) <> 0)
End Function

Private Function KC28_LogOn(ByVal category As String) As Boolean
    KC28_LogOn = True
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    If gKC28.Exists("Service.LogEnabled") Then
        If Not CBool(gKC28("Service.LogEnabled")) Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    End If
    If gKC28.Exists(category) Then KC28_LogOn = CBool(gKC28(category))
End Function

Private Function KC28_Number(ByVal text As String) As Double
    Dim re As Object, value As String
    value = Replace(Trim$(text), ",", ".")
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "^[+-]?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?([Ee][+-]?[0-9]+)?$"
    If Not re.Test(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3452, , "Îæèäàåòñÿ ÷èñëî: " & text
    KC28_Number = val(value)
End Function

Private Sub KC28_Def(ByVal defs As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal kind As String, ByVal minimum As Double, ByVal maximum As Double, Optional ByVal displayScale As Double = 1, Optional ByVal defaultValue As Variant)
    ' v16.2: defaultValue çàäàí -> ñòðîêó ìîæíî íå çàâîäèòü â Excel (èëè îñòàâèòü çíà÷åíèå ïóñòûì).
    Dim d As Variant
    If IsMissing(defaultValue) Then d = Empty Else d = defaultValue
    defs.Add key, Array(kind, minimum, maximum, displayScale, d)
End Sub

    End If

    If schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3455, , "Íåïîääåðæèâàåìàÿ âåðñèÿ íàñòðîåê."
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): seen.CompareMode = 1
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then

    Else
        pageList = Array("Ôàéëû è ïàïêè", "Áàëëîíû", "Íîðìû êîíòðîëÿ", "Øèôðû", "Ïîäïèñè", "Ñåðâèñ è ëîãè", "Òàáëèöû ÎÊ", "Òàáëèöû ÊÝ", "Ðàìêè ÎÊ", "Ðàìêè ÊÝ", "?Ñâîè ïàðàìåòðû")
    End If
    For Each pageName In pageList
        If Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then
            ' Íåîáÿçàòåëüíûé ëèñò: íåò åãî èëè îí ïóñò — íå îøèáêà.
            If Not KC28_HasSheet(book, Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2)) Then GoTo NextPage
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2))
        Else
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(CStr(pageName))
        End If
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)).End(-4162).row
        If last < 2 And Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then GoTo NextPage
        If last < 2 Or last > 5000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3456, , "Íåêîððåêòíûé ëèñò íàñòðîåê: " & ws.name
        values = ws.Range("A2:F" & CStr(last)).Value2
        For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
            keyColumn = IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)
            If IsError(values(r, keyColumn)) Or IsError(values(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Îøèáêà Excel: " & ws.name & ", ñòðîêà " & CStr(r + 1)
            key = Trim$(CStr(values(r, keyColumn))): value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 2)))
            location = ws.name & "!B" & CStr(r + 1)
            If Len(key) = 0 And Len(value) = 0 Then GoTo NextRow   ' v16.2: ïóñòàÿ ñòðîêà-ðàçäåëèòåëü
            If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3458, , "Ïóñòîé ñëóæåáíûé êëþ÷: " & location
            If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3460, , "Ïàðàìåòð ïîâòîðÿåòñÿ: " & location
            If Not defs.Exists(key) Then
                ' v16.2: ñâîé ïàðàìåòð — íå îøèáêà. Õðàíèòñÿ êàê òåêñò, ÷èòàåòñÿ KC28_S("êëþ÷").
                seen.Add key, value
                If Left$(key, 5) <> "Ñâîè." And Left$(key, 5) <> "User." Then _
                    gKC28Warn = gKC28Warn & "• " & key & " (" & location & ")" & vbCrLf
                GoTo NextRow
            End If
            spec = defs(key): kind = CStr(spec(0))
            If Len(value) = 0 And Not IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then GoTo NextRow   ' âîçüì¸òñÿ çíà÷åíèå ïî óìîë÷àíèþ
            If Len(value) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3461, , "Çàïîëíèòå çíà÷åíèå: " & location
            If kind = "String" Then
                If Left$(key, 6) = "Codes." Then
                    If Len(value) <> 5 Or value Like "*[!0-9]*" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3462, , "Øèôð äîëæåí ñîäåðæàòü ïÿòü öèôð: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, value
            Else
                If kind = "Boolean" Then n = KC32_Flag(value) Else n = KC28_Number(value)
                If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then n = n * CDbl(spec(3))
                If n < CDbl(spec(1)) Or n > CDbl(spec(2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3463, , "Çíà÷åíèå âíå äîïóñòèìîãî äèàïàçîíà: " & location
                If kind = "Long" Or kind = "Boolean" Then
                    If n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3464, , "Ââåäèòå öåëîå çíà÷åíèå: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, n
            End If
NextRow:
        Next r
NextPage:
    Next pageName
    ' Ñòàðûé ôàéë ñîõðàíÿåò ïîëüçîâàòåëüñêèå çíà÷åíèÿ; íîâûé ïàðàìåòð èìååò ñîâìåñòèìûé default.
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And Not seen.Exists("Codes.CompactPrefixLength") Then seen.Add "Codes.CompactPrefixLength", 4
    For Each defKey In defs.Keys
        If Not seen.Exists(CStr(defKey)) Then
            spec = defs(CStr(defKey))
            If IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3465, , "Ïðîïóùåí ïàðàìåòð " & CStr(defKey) & ". Âîññòàíîâèòå ñòðîêó èç íîâîãî ôàéëà íàñòðîåê."
            If CStr(spec(0)) = "String" Then seen.Add CStr(defKey), CStr(spec(4)) Else seen.Add CStr(defKey), CDbl(spec(4))
        End If
    Next defKey
    Set gKC28 = seen
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Ñðåäñòâà ÒÎ")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Íåêîððåêòíîå ÷èñëî ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Enabled" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: Enabled"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Type" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: Type"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "StandardContains" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: StandardContains"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 4).Value2) <> "WeldPrefixes" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: WeldPrefixes"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 5).Value2) <> "GdtEquals" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: GdtEquals"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 6).Value2) <> "LegMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: LegMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 7).Value2) <> "RoundMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: RoundMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 8).Value2) <> "RoundMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: RoundMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 9).Value2) <> "RoundedLegs" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: RoundedLegs"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 10).Value2) <> "Parity" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: Parity"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 11).Value2) <> "NominalMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: NominalMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 12).Value2) <> "NominalMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: NominalMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 13).Value2) <> "MainTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: MainTool"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 14).Value2) <> "ExtraTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Èçìåí¸í çàãîëîâîê ïðàâèë ñðåäñòâ ÒÎ: ExtraTool"
    gKC28Rules = KC28_Widen(ws.Range("A2:N" & CStr(last)).Value2)
    Else
        ' v16.2: ñòîëáöû èùóòñÿ ïî çàãîëîâêàì; ïîðÿäîê ñâîáîäíûé, ëèøíèå ñòîëáöû (êîììåíòàðèè) èãíîðèðóþòñÿ,
        ' «Ïðåðûâèñòûé øîâ» è «Òåêñò ñîäåðæèò» — íåîáÿçàòåëüíûå ñòîëáöû.
        Set ws = book.Worksheets("Âûáîð èíñòðóìåíòîâ")
        gKC28Rules = KC28_ReadRuleTable(ws, "Îñíîâíîå ñðåäñòâî", "Äîïîëíèòåëüíîå ñðåäñòâî")
        If IsEmpty(gKC28Rules) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Ëèñò «Âûáîð èíñòðóìåíòîâ» ïóñò."
    End If
    KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Rules
    KC28_ValidateRules
    ' v16.2: íåîáÿçàòåëüíûé ëèñò — ÊÀÆÄÀß ïîäõîäÿùàÿ ñòðîêà äîáàâëÿåò ñâî¸ ñðåäñòâî.
    If KC28_HasSheet(book, "Äîáàâî÷íûå ñðåäñòâà") Then
        gKC28Extra = KC28_ReadRuleTable(book.Worksheets("Äîáàâî÷íûå ñðåäñòâà"), "Äîáàâèòü ñðåäñòâî", "")
        If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Extra) Then
            KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Extra
            KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Extra, "Äîáàâî÷íûå ñðåäñòâà", False
        End If
    End If
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Øàáëîíû ðàçìåðîâ")
    If (CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Ñòàíäàðò" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Standard") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Òèï øâà" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "WeldType") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Íàäïèñü â ãðàôå Ðàçìåðû" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Template") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3483, , "Èçìåíåíû çàãîëîâêè øàáëîíîâ ðàçìåðîâ."
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3484, , "Íåêîððåêòíàÿ òàáëèöà øàáëîíîâ ðàçìåðîâ."
    values = ws.Range("A2:C" & CStr(last)).Value2
    Set gKC28Sizes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): gKC28Sizes.CompareMode = 1
    For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
        For c = 1 To 3
            If IsError(values(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3485, , "Îøèáêà Excel â øàáëîíå ðàçìåðîâ."
        Next c
        value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 1)))
        If value <> "14771" And value <> "23518" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3486, , "Standard øàáëîíà: 14771 èëè 23518."
        key = value & "|" & KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))
        If Len(KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3487, , "Ïóñòîé òèï øâà â øàáëîíå ðàçìåðîâ."
        If gKC28Sizes.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3488, , "Ïîâòîð øàáëîíà ðàçìåðîâ " & key
        value = Replace(CStr(values(r, 3)), Chr(13), "")
        value = Replace(value, "|", Chr(10))
        value = Replace(value, "… ", "")
        value = Replace(value, "…", "")
        gKC28Sizes.Add key, value
    Next r
    If owned Then book.Close False: xl.Quit
    Set book = Nothing: Set xl = Nothing
    OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH = KC28_S("M2.OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH")
    OK1_TABLE_X = KC28_N("M2.OK1_TABLE_X")

    errorText = "Íàñòðîéêè: " & Err.description

        If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
        If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRules()
    KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Rules, "Âûáîð èíñòðóìåíòîâ", True
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRuleTable(ByRef rules As Variant, ByVal label As String, ByVal requireFallback As Boolean)
    Dim re As Object, at As String
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, n As Double, text As String, parts As Variant, p As Variant
    Dim haveFallback As Boolean
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        at = " (" & label & ", ñòðîêà " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")"
        For c = 1 To 16
            If IsError(rules(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Îøèáêà Excel â ïðàâèëå" & at
            If VarType(rules(r, c)) = vbString Then rules(r, c) = Trim$(CStr(rules(r, c)))
        Next c
        n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        If n <> 0 And n <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3471, , "Â êîëîíêå «Èñïîëüçîâàòü» âûáåðèòå Äà èëè Íåò."
        text = CStr(rules(r, 2))
        If InStr("|DIMENSION|GTOL|WELD|NOTE|*|", "|" & text & "|") = 0 Or Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3472, , "Íåâåðíîå çíà÷åíèå «×òî êîíòðîëèðóåì»" & at
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 13))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3473, , "Íå çàäàíî ñðåäñòâî ÒÎ" & at
        For c = 6 To 8
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Íåâåðíàÿ ãðàíèöà êàòåòà â ïðàâèëå."
                If c > 6 And n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Ãðàíèöû îêðóãë¸ííîãî êàòåòà äîëæíû áûòü öåëûìè."
            End If
        Next c
        For c = 11 To 12
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Íåâåðíàÿ ãðàíèöà íîìèíàëà â ïðàâèëå."
            End If
        Next c
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 8))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 8))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Íèæíÿÿ ãðàíèöà êàòåòà áîëüøå âåðõíåé."
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 12))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 12))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Íèæíÿÿ ãðàíèöà íîìèíàëà áîëüøå âåðõíåé."
        End If
        text = CStr(rules(r, 10))
        If text <> "" And text <> "ODD" And text <> "EVEN" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3475, , "×¸òíîñòü êàòåòà: ïóñòî, Íå÷¸òíûé èëè ×¸òíûé."
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 9))) > 0 Then
            parts = Split(CStr(rules(r, 9)), ",")
            For Each p In parts
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(p))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Or n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3476, , "Â ñïèñêå êàòåòîâ íóæíû öåëûå çíà÷åíèÿ ÷åðåç çàïÿòóþ."
            Next p
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 16))) > 0 Then
            Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
            re.Pattern = CStr(rules(r, 16))
            On Error Resume Next
            re.Test "x"
            If Err.number <> 0 Then
                Err.Clear
                On Error GoTo 0
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "Íåâåðíîå âûðàæåíèå â «Òåêñò ñîäåðæèò»" & at
            End If
            On Error GoTo 0
        End If
        If CStr(rules(r, 2)) = "*" And CStr(rules(r, 1)) = "1" Then
            text = ""
            For c = 3 To 12: text = text & CStr(rules(r, c)): Next c
            text = text & CStr(rules(r, 15)) & CStr(rules(r, 16))
            If Len(text) = 0 Then haveFallback = True
        End If
    Next r
    If requireFallback And Not haveFallback Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3477, , "Íóæíî âêëþ÷¸ííîå çàïàñíîå ïðàâèëî «Ëþáîé» áåç óñëîâèé."
End Sub


' Ïåðåïðèâÿçêà âûçûâàåòñÿ òîëüêî åñëè ñîõðàí¸ííûé/îæèäàåìûé ôàéë îòñóòñòâóåò.
' Íèêàêèõ îïåðàöèé íàä àííîòàöèÿìè, áàëëîíàìè èëè ãåîìåòðèåé â ýòèõ ïðîöåäóðàõ íåò.
Private Sub KC30_WriteBinding(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal path As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, target As String, temp As String, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    target = drawing & ".okintro-v12.txt": temp = target & ".tmp"
    Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(temp, True, True)
    stream.WriteLine "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12"
    stream.WriteLine drawing
    stream.WriteLine path
    stream.Close
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(temp, 1, False, -1)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Îøèáêà çàïèñè ïðèâÿçêè."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Îøèáêà âëàäåëüöà ïðèâÿçêè."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, path, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Îøèáêà ïóòè ïðèâÿçêè."
    stream.Close
    fso.CopyFile temp, target, True
    fso.DeleteFile temp
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    If Not fso Is Nothing Then
        If Len(temp) > 0 Then
            If fso.FileExists(temp) Then fso.DeleteFile temp
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3501, "KC30_WriteBinding", "Íå çàïèñàíà ïðèâÿçêà: " & detail & ". Áàëëîíû íå èçìåíåíû."
End Sub

Private Sub KC30_ValidateSource(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As String)
    Dim card As Object, raw As Object, meta As Object, app As Object, model As Object
    Dim sheets As Variant, known As Object, data As Variant, last As Long, r As Long, i As Long
    Set card = book.Worksheets("Êàðòà êîíòðîëÿ")
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(card) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3502, , "Íå ðàñïîçíàíà ïîëíàÿ êàðòà êîíòðîëÿ."
    Set meta = book.Worksheets("ÎÊ_Ñëóæåáíîå")
    If Len(Trim$(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3503, , "Âûáðàí ÷èñòûé øàáëîí, à íå ðàáî÷àÿ êàðòà."
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà")
    If CStr(raw.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "¹ ïîç." Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Òåêñò àííîòàöèè" Or _
       CStr(raw.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Ëèñò" Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "×åðò¸æíûé âèä" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3504, , "Íå ðàñïîçíàíà ñòðóêòóðà èñõîäíûõ äàííûõ êàðòû."
    last = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3505, , "Â êàðòå íåò êîððåêòíîãî ñïèñêà ïàðàìåòðîâ."
    Set app = Application.SldWorks: Set model = app.ActiveDoc
    If StrComp(CStr(model.GetPathName), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3506, , "Àêòèâíûé ÷åðò¸æ ñìåíèëñÿ."
    sheets = model.GetSheetNames
    Set known = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): known.CompareMode = 1
    For i = LBound(sheets) To UBound(sheets): known(CStr(sheets(i))) = True: Next i
    data = raw.Range("W2:X" & CStr(last)).Value2
    For r = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        If IsError(data(r, 1)) Or IsError(data(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3507, , "Îøèáêà Excel â èñõîäíûõ äàííûõ."
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3508, , "Ïóñòîå èìÿ èñõîäíîãî ëèñòà."
        If Not known.Exists(CStr(data(r, 1))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3509, , "Â òåêóùåì ÷åðòåæå íåò èñõîäíîãî ëèñòà: " & CStr(data(r, 1)) & ". Òðåáóåòñÿ îòäåëüíûé ðàçáîð ñîîòâåòñòâèé, ïðèâÿçêà íå èçìåíåíà."
    Next r
End Sub

Private Function KC30_RebindMissing(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal expected As String, ByVal required As Boolean) As String
    Dim answer As VbMsgBoxResult, xl As Object, book As Object, sourceBook As Object, picked As Variant, dest As Variant
    Dim fso As Object, oldOwner As String, target As String, meta As Object, item As Variant, text As String
    Dim existingApp As Object, openBook As Object, detail As String, sourcePath As String
    answer = MsgBox("Ñâÿçàííàÿ ðàáî÷àÿ êàðòà íå íàéäåíà:" & vbCrLf & expected & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Äà — âûáðàòü ñóùåñòâóþùóþ .xlsm. Äëÿ äðóãîãî ÷åðòåæà áóäåò ñîçäàíà îòäåëüíàÿ êîïèÿ; èñõîäíàÿ êàðòà íå èçìåíèòñÿ." & vbCrLf & _
        "Íåò — íîâàÿ êàðòà ïåðâûì ìàêðîñîì." & vbCrLf & "Îòìåíà — îñòàíîâèòüñÿ áåç èçìåíåíèÿ áàëëîíîâ.", vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "Êàðòà êîíòðîëÿ íå íàéäåíà")
    If answer = vbCancel Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Âûáîð ðàáî÷åé êàðòû îòìåí¸í. Áàëëîíû íå èçìåíåíû."
    If answer = vbNo Then
        If required Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3511, , "Ñîçäàéòå íîâóþ êàðòó ïåðâûì ìàêðîñîì."
        target = KC14_DefaultPath(drawing)
        If CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Íîâàÿ êíèãà ïî óìîë÷àíèþ óæå ñóùåñòâóåò. Âûáåðèòå Äà è óêàæèòå å¸ ÿâíî. Ïåðåçàïèñè íåò."
        KC30_RebindMissing = target
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application"): xl.Visible = True
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
    picked = KC32_PickCard(xl, drawing, "Âûáåðèòå ñóùåñòâóþùóþ ïîëíóþ êàðòó", "*.xlsm")
    If VarType(picked) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Âûáîð êàðòû îòìåí¸í."
    sourcePath = CStr(picked)
    If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(sourcePath)) <> "xlsm" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3512, , "Íóæíà ðàáî÷àÿ .xlsm."
    On Error Resume Next
    Set existingApp = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not existingApp Is Nothing Then
        For Each openBook In existingApp.Workbooks
            If StrComp(openBook.FullName, sourcePath, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                If Not openBook.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3513, , "Ñíà÷àëà ñîõðàíèòå âûáðàííóþ êàðòó Excel."
            End If
        Next openBook
    End If
    Set sourceBook = xl.Workbooks.Open(sourcePath, 0, True)
    KC30_ValidateSource sourceBook, drawing
    Set meta = sourceBook.Worksheets("ÎÊ_Ñëóæåáíîå")
    oldOwner = CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2)
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        target = sourcePath
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
    Else
        dest = xl.GetSaveAsFilename(KC14_DefaultPath(drawing), "Excel ñ ìàêðîñàìè (*.xlsm),*.xlsm", , "Íåçàâèñèìàÿ êîïèÿ êàðòû äëÿ òåêóùåãî ÷åðòåæà")
        If VarType(dest) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Ñîçäàíèå êîïèè îòìåíåíî."
        target = CStr(dest)
        If LCase$(Right$(target, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then target = target & ".xlsm"
        If fso.FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Ôàéë óæå ñóùåñòâóåò. Âûáåðèòå íîâîå èìÿ, ïåðåçàïèñü çàïðåùåíà."
        sourceBook.SaveCopyAs target
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(target, 0, False)
        If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3515, , "Êîïèÿ äîñòóïíà òîëüêî äëÿ ÷òåíèÿ."
        Set meta = book.Worksheets("ÎÊ_Ñëóæåáíîå")
        meta.Range("A12").Value2 = "CardCopyId"
        meta.Range("B12").Value2 = Format$(Now, "yyyymmddhhnnss") & "_" & fso.GetTempName
        meta.Range("A13").Value2 = "CopiedFrom"
        meta.Range("B13").Value2 = sourcePath
        KC37_InvalidateCopiedLayout book, oldOwner, drawing
        meta.Range("B2").Value2 = drawing
        meta.Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
        book.Save
        book.Close False: Set book = Nothing
        For Each item In Array("Sheets", "SheetsKnown", "Types", "Anchor")
            text = KC14_Setting(oldOwner, CStr(item), "")
            If Len(text) > 0 Then KC14_SaveSetting drawing, CStr(item), text
        Next item
    End If
    xl.Quit: Set xl = Nothing
    KC30_WriteBinding drawing, target
    KC14_Log drawing, "Ïåðåïðèâÿçêà: " & sourcePath & " -> " & target
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        MsgBox "Íåçàâèñèìàÿ êîïèÿ êàðòû ñîçäàíà è ïðèâÿçàíà. Èñõîäíàÿ êíèãà è áàëëîíû íå èçìåíåíû." & vbCrLf & _
        "Ïåðåä çàïîëíåíèåì ÷åðòåæà âûïîëíèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà: îí ñâåðèò ïàðàìåòðû, èñïîëüçóÿ ñóùåñòâóþùèå áàëëîíû.", vbInformation
    KC30_RebindMissing = target
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3516, "KC30_RebindMissing", detail & vbCrLf & "Ïðèâÿçêà íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà; áàëëîíû íå èçìåíåíû. Ñîçäàííàÿ êîïèÿ, åñëè åñòü, îñòàâëåíà íà äèñêå."
End Function


Private Function KC28_WeldKey(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim text As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    text = Replace(text, "C", "Ñ")
    text = Replace(text, "T", "Ò")
    text = Replace(text, "H", "Í")
    text = Replace(text, "Y", "Ó")
    KC28_WeldKey = text
End Function


' v19: ñåìàíòè÷åñêîå ÷òåíèå ñòàðîé è íîâîé ïàíåëè áåç ïîäìåíû çíà÷åíèé ìåñòàìè.
Private Function KC29_NewFields(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    KC29_NewFields = (KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå" And KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Îáîðóäîâàíèå ÎÊ1")
End Function

Private Function KC29_TextField(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal name As String, ByVal address As String) As String
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names(name)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then
        Set area = ws.Range(address)
    Else
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3400, , "Äèàïàçîí " & name & " îòíîñèòñÿ ê äðóãîìó ëèñòó."
        If area.address <> ws.Range(address).address Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3401, , "Äèàïàçîí " & name & " äîëæåí óêàçûâàòü íà " & address & ". Âûïîëíèòå OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    If area.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3402, , "Íå îáúåäèíÿéòå ÿ÷åéêó " & address & "."
    KC29_TextField = KC23_CellText(area)
End Function

Private Function KC27_ReadPrimary(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef value As String) As Boolean
    value = ""
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then Exit Function
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå" Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "I18")
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC27_ReadPrimary = True
End Function

Private Function KC29_ReadHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef kd As String, ByRef itemName As String, ByRef equipment As String) As Boolean
    kd = "": itemName = "": equipment = ""
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) <> "Ðåêâèçèòû èçäåëèÿ" Then Exit Function
    kd = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4")
    itemName = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5")
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Îáîðóäîâàíèå ÎÊ1" Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "L6")
    Else
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3403, , "Íå ðàñïîçíàíà ïàíåëü ðåêâèçèòîâ. Âûïîëíèòå OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    KC29_ReadHeaderData = True
End Function

Private Sub KC29_EnsureHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim kd As String, itemName As String, equipment As String, primary As String
    Dim haveProduct As Boolean, havePrimary As Boolean, cell As Object, fresh As Boolean
    ' Äî ïåðâîé çàïèñè ÷èòàåì îáà ñòàðûõ ïîëÿ. Ïîâòîðíîå îáíîâëåíèå íå ìåíÿåò èõ ìåñòàìè.
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(ws, kd, itemName, equipment)
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(ws, primary)
    For Each cell In ws.Range("K3:L6").cells
        If cell.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Îñâîáîäèòå K3:L6 îò îáúåäèíåíèé ïåðåä îáíîâëåíèåì ïàíåëè."
    Next cell
    If ws.Range("H18").MergeCells Or ws.Range("I18").MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Îñâîáîäèòå H18:I18 îò îáúåäèíåíèé."
    If Not haveProduct Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("K3:L6")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3405, , "Â K3:L6 åñòü ïîñòîðîííèå äàííûå. Ïåðåíåñèòå èõ äî îáíîâëåíèÿ."
    End If
    If Not havePrimary And Not KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("H18:I18")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3406, , "Â H18:I18 åñòü ïîñòîðîííèå äàííûå. Ïåðåíåñèòå èõ äî îáíîâëåíèÿ."
    End If
    ws.Range("K3").Value2 = "Ðåêâèçèòû èçäåëèÿ"
    ws.Range("K4").Value2 = "Íîìåð ÊÄ"
    ws.Range("K5").Value2 = "Íàèìåíîâàíèå èçäåëèÿ"
    ws.Range("K6").Value2 = "Ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå"
    ws.Range("H18").Value2 = "Îáîðóäîâàíèå ÎÊ1"
    ws.Range("L4:L6").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("I18").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("L4").Value2 = kd
    ws.Range("L5").Value2 = itemName
    ws.Range("L6").Value2 = primary
    ws.Range("I18").Value2 = equipment
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductData", "L4:L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18"
    ws.Columns("K").ColumnWidth = 26
    ws.Columns("L").ColumnWidth = 60
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("K4:L6").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("K4:L6").WrapText = True
    ws.Range("L4:L6").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
    ws.Range("H18").WrapText = True
    With ws.Range("I18")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Bold = False
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=2
    End With
    If ws.rows(5).RowHeight < 40 Then ws.rows(5).RowHeight = 40
    If ws.rows(18).RowHeight < 30 Then ws.rows(18).RowHeight = 30
End Sub


Private Sub KC31_AddIssue(ByRef issues As String, ByVal text As String, ByRef firstAddress As String, ByVal address As String)
    issues = issues & ChrW(&H2022) & " " & text & vbCrLf
    If Len(firstAddress) = 0 And Len(address) > 0 Then firstAddress = address
End Sub

Private Function KC31_ReadCell(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal address As String, ByVal label As String, ByRef issues As String, ByRef firstAddress As String, ByRef valid As Boolean) As String
    Dim text As String, detail As String
    valid = False
    On Error GoTo Failed
    text = KC23_CellText(ws.Range(address))
    valid = True
    KC31_ReadCell = Trim$(Replace(text, ChrW(160), " "))
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    KC31_AddIssue issues, label & " (" & address & "): " & detail, firstAddress, address
End Function

Private Function KC31_ValidateCard(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object, ByRef primary As String, ByRef kd As String, ByRef itemName As String) As Boolean
    Dim issues As String, focus As String, raw As String, n As Double, valid As Boolean
    Dim role As String, surname As String, foundDeveloper As Boolean, r As Long, readOnlyText As String
    primary = "": kd = "": itemName = "": gKC29Equipment = ""
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(ws) Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íå ðàñïîçíàíà ñòðóêòóðà êàðòû; îáíîâèòå å¸ ïåðâûì ìàêðîñîì.", focus, "L4"
    If Not KC29_NewFields(ws) Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Îáíîâèòå Excel-ìîäóëü äî v19 è âûïîëíèòå OK_UpdateEditor äëÿ ïåðåíîñà ïîëåé.", focus, "L6"
    raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L4", "Íîìåð ÊÄ", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid Then
        kd = NormalizePartDesignation(raw)
        If Len(raw) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íå çàïîëíåí íîìåð ÊÄ (L4).", focus, "L4"
        ElseIf Len(kd) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íåâåðíûé íîìåð ÊÄ (L4). Ïðèìåðû: D10M.1133.000 èëè 1211002020.", focus, "L4"
        ElseIf Len(BuildShiftedCode(kd, KC28_S("Codes.20102"))) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Â íîìåðå ÊÄ (L4) íåäîñòàòî÷íî öèôð äëÿ ôîðìèðîâàíèÿ îáîçíà÷åíèÿ.", focus, "L4"
        End If
    End If
    itemName = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L5", "Íàèìåíîâàíèå èçäåëèÿ", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid And Len(itemName) = 0 Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íå çàïîëíåíî íàèìåíîâàíèå èçäåëèÿ (L5).", focus, "L5"
    ' Ñòàðóþ ïàíåëü íå ÷èòàåì êàê íîâóþ: L6 ðàíüøå ñîäåðæàëà îáîðóäîâàíèå.
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L6", "Ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå", issues, focus, valid)
        If valid Then
            primary = NormalizePartDesignation(raw)
            If Len(raw) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íå çàïîëíåíî ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå (L6).", focus, "L6"
            ElseIf Len(primary) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íåâåðíîå ïåðâè÷íîå ïðèìåíåíèå (L6). Ïðèìåðû: D10M.00.000-02 èëè 1211002000.", focus, "L6"
            ElseIf Len(BuildShiftedCode(primary, KC28_S("Codes.02190"))) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "Â ïåðâè÷íîì ïðèìåíåíèè (L6) íåäîñòàòî÷íî öèôð.", focus, "L6"
            End If
        End If
        gKC29Equipment = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I18", "Îáîðóäîâàíèå ÎÊ1", issues, focus, valid)
        ' Ïóñòîå îáîðóäîâàíèå äîïóñòèìî. Îøèáêà Excel â í¸ì — íå ïóñòîå çíà÷åíèå.
    End If
    raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I17", "Ìàññà", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid Then
        If Not NumericCellValue(raw, n) Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Ìàññà (I17) äîëæíà áûòü çàïîëíåíà ïîëîæèòåëüíûì ÷èñëîì.", focus, "I17"
        ElseIf n <= 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Ìàññà (I17) äîëæíà áûòü áîëüøå íóëÿ.", focus, "I17"
        End If
    End If
    For r = 4 To 8
        role = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "H" & CStr(r), "Äîëæíîñòü", issues, focus, valid)
        If valid Then
            role = LCase$(Replace(Replace(role, ".", ""), " ", ""))
            If role = "ðàçðàá" Then
                foundDeveloper = True
                surname = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I" & CStr(r), "Ôàìèëèÿ Ðàçðàá.", issues, focus, valid)
                If valid And Len(surname) = 0 Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Íå çàïîëíåíà ôàìèëèÿ íàïðîòèâ «Ðàçðàá.» (I" & CStr(r) & ").", focus, "I" & CStr(r)
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not foundDeveloper Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Â H4:H8 íå íàéäåíà ñòðîêà «Ðàçðàá.» äëÿ ïðîâåðêè ôàìèëèè.", focus, "H4"
    If Len(issues) = 0 Then KC31_ValidateCard = True: Exit Function
    On Error Resume Next
    xl.Visible = True
    If book.ReadOnly Then book.ChangeFileAccess 2, , False
    If book.ReadOnly Then readOnlyText = vbCrLf & "Êíèãà òîëüêî äëÿ ÷òåíèÿ: îòêðîéòå èñõîäíóþ .xlsm îáû÷íûì ñïîñîáîì äëÿ ñîõðàíåíèÿ."
    book.Activate: ws.Activate
    If Len(focus) > 0 Then ws.Range(focus).Select
    On Error GoTo 0
    TopMsg "Èñïðàâüòå âñå ïåðå÷èñëåííûå ïîëÿ:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & issues & readOnlyText & vbCrLf & _
        "Ñîõðàíèòå êíèãó è ïîâòîðèòå çàïóñê. ×åðò¸æ íå èçìåí¸í.", "Ïðîâåðêà êàðòû êîíòðîëÿ", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
End Function


Private Function KC32_PickCard(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal drawing As String, ByVal title As String, ByVal filter As String) As Variant
    Dim dialog As Object, fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.GetParentFolderName(drawing)
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3550, , "Ïàïêà àêòèâíîãî äîêóìåíòà íåäîñòóïíà: " & folder
    Set dialog = xl.FileDialog(3)
    With dialog
        .title = title
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .filters.Clear
        .filters.Add "Êàðòû êîíòðîëÿ Excel", filter
        .InitialFileName = folder & "\"
        KC32_PickCard = False
        If .Show = -1 Then KC32_PickCard = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
    End With
End Function


Private Function KC32_Flag(ByVal value As String) As Double
    Select Case UCase$(Trim$(value))
        Case "ÄÀ": KC32_Flag = 1
        Case "ÍÅÒ": KC32_Flag = 0
        Case Else: KC32_Flag = KC28_Number(value)
    End Select
End Function

' Óáèðàåì òîëüêî ñèìâîë-çàãëóøêó â e=… íå áîëåå / e1=… íå ìåíåå.
' ×èñëîâûå ðàçìåðû, ðó÷íîé òåêñò äðóãèõ âèäîâ è èçîáðàæåíèÿ îñòàþòñÿ ïðåæíèìè.
Private Function KC33_CompactSizeNotation(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = True
    re.Pattern = "([eå][0-9]*=)[ ]*(…|\.{3})[ ]*(íå[ ]+(áîëåå|ìåíåå))"
    KC33_CompactSizeNotation = re.Replace(text, "$1$3")
End Function

Private Sub KC33_RefreshSizeNotation(ByVal drawing As Object, ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, ByVal count As Long)
    Dim originalSheet As String, table As Object, page As Long, pages As Long, perPage As Long
    Dim firstIdx As Long, lastIdx As Long, rowsData As Long, i As Long, localIdx As Long, row As Long, col As Long
    Dim oldText As String, newText As String, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If count <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    originalSheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    perPage = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    pages = (count + perPage - 1) \ perPage
    For page = 1 To pages
        firstIdx = (page - 1) * perPage + 1
        lastIdx = firstIdx + perPage - 1
        If lastIdx > count Then lastIdx = count
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, "ÊÝ (Øâû Ëèñò " & CStr(page) & ")") Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Íå àêòèâèðîâàí ëèñò äëÿ èñïðàâëåíèÿ ðàçìåðíîé íàäïèñè."
        Set table = KE_OneWeldTableOnSheet(drawing)
        rowsData = (lastIdx - firstIdx + 1 + KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ KE_GROUPS_COUNT
        If table.rowCount <> rowsData + 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Èçìåíèëàñü ñòðóêòóðà òàáëèöû øâîâ."
        For i = firstIdx To lastIdx
            If KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Then
                localIdx = i - firstIdx
                row = (localIdx Mod rowsData) + 1
                col = (localIdx \ rowsData) * 4
                If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(welds(i).NumberLabel) Or _
                   KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 1, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(BuildWeldDesignation(welds(i))) Or _
                   KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(0, col + 3, False))) <> "Ðàçìåðû" Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà ÿ÷åéêà ðàçìåðíîé íàäïèñè."
                oldText = CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 3, False))
                newText = KC33_CompactSizeNotation(oldText)
                If newText <> oldText Then
                    table.Text2(row, col + 3, False) = newText
                    If CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 3, False)) <> newText Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíî èñïðàâëåíèå ðàçìåðíîé íàäïèñè."
                End If
            End If
        Next i
    Next page
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, "KC33_RefreshSizeNotation", detail
End Sub




Private Function KC28_HasSheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(ws.name)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then KC28_HasSheet = True: Exit Function
    Next ws
End Function


Private Function KC28_HeaderKey(ByVal value As Variant) As String
    Dim t As String
    If IsError(value) Then Exit Function
    t = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(value)))
    t = Replace(t, "¸", "å")
    t = Replace(t, ChrW(160), " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbCr, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbLf, " ")
    Do While InStr(t, "  ") > 0: t = Replace(t, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC28_HeaderKey = t
End Function


Private Function KC28_ReadRuleTable(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal toolHeader As String, ByVal extraHeader As String) As Variant
    Dim names As Variant, cols(1 To 16) As Long, lastCol As Long, lastRow As Long, rowLast As Long
    Dim c As Long, k As Long, r As Long, n As Long, h As String, v As Variant
    Dim values As Variant, result As Variant, blank As Boolean
    names = Array("Èñïîëüçîâàòü", "×òî êîíòðîëèðóåì", "ÃÎÑÒ ñîäåðæèò", "Òèïû øâîâ", "Ãåîìåòðè÷åñêèé äîïóñê", _
        "Êàòåò îò, ìì", "Îêðóãë¸ííûé êàòåò îò", "Îêðóãë¸ííûé êàòåò äî", "Êàòåòû èç ñïèñêà", "×¸òíîñòü êàòåòà", _
        "Íîìèíàë îò, ìì", "Íîìèíàë äî, ìì", toolHeader, extraHeader, "Ïðåðûâèñòûé øîâ", "Òåêñò ñîäåðæèò")
    lastCol = ws.cells(1, ws.Columns.count).End(-4159).Column
    For c = 1 To lastCol
        h = KC28_HeaderKey(ws.cells(1, c).Value2)
        If Len(h) > 0 Then
            For k = 1 To 16
                If Len(CStr(names(k - 1))) > 0 Then
                    If h = KC28_HeaderKey(names(k - 1)) Then
                        If cols(k) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Ñòîëáåö «" & names(k - 1) & "» ïîâòîðÿåòñÿ íà ëèñòå " & ws.name
                        cols(k) = c
                    End If
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next c
    If cols(1) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Ëèñò «" & ws.name & "»: íåò ñòîëáöà «Èñïîëüçîâàòü»."
    If cols(2) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Ëèñò «" & ws.name & "»: íåò ñòîëáöà «×òî êîíòðîëèðóåì»."
    If cols(13) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Ëèñò «" & ws.name & "»: íåò ñòîëáöà «" & toolHeader & "»."
    lastRow = 1
    For k = 1 To 16
        If cols(k) > 0 Then
            rowLast = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, cols(k)).End(-4162).row
            If rowLast > lastRow Then lastRow = rowLast
        End If
    Next k
    If lastRow > 1001 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Ñëèøêîì ìíîãî ïðàâèë íà ëèñòå " & ws.name
    If lastRow < 2 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    values = ws.Range(ws.cells(2, 1), ws.cells(lastRow, lastCol)).Value2
    ReDim result(1 To lastRow - 1, 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To lastRow - 1
        blank = True
        For k = 1 To 16
            If cols(k) > 0 Then
                v = values(r, cols(k))
                If IsError(v) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Îøèáêà Excel: " & ws.name & ", ñòðîêà " & CStr(r + 1)
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(v))) > 0 Then blank = False
            End If
        Next k
        If Not blank Then          ' ïóñòûå ñòðîêè-ðàçäåëèòåëè ðàçðåøåíû
            n = n + 1
            For k = 1 To 16
                If cols(k) > 0 Then result(n, k) = Trim$(CStr(values(r, cols(k)))) Else result(n, k) = ""
            Next k
            result(n, 17) = r + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If n = 0 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    KC28_ReadRuleTable = KC28_Shrink(result, n, 17)
End Function


Private Function KC28_Shrink(ByRef src As Variant, ByVal n As Long, ByVal width As Long) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To n, 1 To width)
    For r = 1 To n
        For c = 1 To width: result(r, c) = src(r, c): Next c
    Next r
    KC28_Shrink = result
End Function


Private Function KC28_Widen(ByVal v As Variant) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To UBound(v, 1), 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To UBound(v, 1)
        For c = 1 To 14: result(r, c) = v(r, c): Next c
        result(r, 15) = "": result(r, 16) = "": result(r, 17) = r + 1
    Next r
    KC28_Widen = result
End Function


Private Sub KC28_NormalizeRules(ByRef rules As Variant)
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        If IsError(rules(r, 1)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Îøèáêà Excel â ïðàâèëå, ñòðîêà " & CStr(rules(r, 17))
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 1)))) = 0 Then rules(r, 1) = 0 Else rules(r, 1) = KC32_Flag(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 2))))
            Case "ÐÀÇÌÅÐ": rules(r, 2) = "DIMENSION"
            Case "ÃÅÎÌÅÒÐÈ×ÅÑÊÈÉ ÄÎÏÓÑÊ": rules(r, 2) = "GTOL"
            Case "ÑÂÀÐÍÎÉ ØÎÂ": rules(r, 2) = "WELD"
            Case "ÇÀÌÅÒÊÀ": rules(r, 2) = "NOTE"
            Case "ËÞÁÎÉ": rules(r, 2) = "*"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 10))))
            Case "ÍÅ×¨ÒÍÛÉ", "ÍÅ×ÅÒÍÛÉ": rules(r, 10) = "ODD"
            Case "×¨ÒÍÛÉ", "×ÅÒÍÛÉ": rules(r, 10) = "EVEN"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 15))))
            Case "": rules(r, 15) = ""
            Case "ÄÀ", "1": rules(r, 15) = "1"
            Case "ÍÅÒ", "0": rules(r, 15) = "0"
            Case Else: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "«Ïðåðûâèñòûé øîâ»: ïóñòî, Äà èëè Íåò (ñòðîêà " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")."
        End Select
    Next r
End Sub


' Êëþ÷è òåêóùåé è ïðåæíåé ãåîìåòðèè îäíîãî è òîãî æå ïîëÿ.
Private Function KC34_IsKEAlias(ByVal marker As String, ByVal wanted As String) As Boolean
    Dim old1 As String, old2 As String
    Select Case wanted
        Case "Field_KE_NAME": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X1, KE1_Y1)
        Case "Field_KE_KD": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X2, KE1_Y2): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X1, KEOTH_Y1)
        Case "Field_KE_PRIMARY": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X3, KE1_Y3): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X2, KEOTH_Y2)
        Case "Field_KE_CODE": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X4, KE1_Y4): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X3, KEOTH_Y3)
        Case "Field_KE_TOTAL": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X5, KE1_Y5)
        Case "Field_KE_PAGE": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X6, KE1_Y6): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X4, KEOTH_Y4)
        Case Else: Exit Function
    End Select
    KC34_IsKEAlias = (Len(marker) > 0 And (marker = old1 Or marker = old2))
End Function

Private Function KC34_FrameKeyMatches(ByVal marker As String, ByVal wanted As String, _
                                     ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double) As Boolean
    KC34_FrameKeyMatches = (StrComp(marker, wanted, vbBinaryCompare) = 0)
    If KC34_FrameKeyMatches Then Exit Function
    If Left$(wanted, 6) = "Field_" Then KC34_FrameKeyMatches = KC34_IsKEAlias(marker, wanted)
End Function

' Èìÿ MF_<ñòàðîå èìÿ ëèñòà>_<X>_<Y>[_N] ïåðåæèâàåò ïåðåèìåíîâàíèå ëèñòà.
' Íå èñïîëüçóåì òîëüêî òåêñò ÿ÷åéêè: ñîâïàäàþùèé òåêñò íå äîêàçûâàåò ïðèíàäëåæíîñòü.
Private Function KC34_LegacyFrameName(ByVal name As String, ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^MF_.+_" & CStr(CLng(xMm * 1000#)) & "_" & CStr(CLng(yMm * 1000#)) & "(_[0-9]+)?$"
    KC34_LegacyFrameName = re.Test(name)
End Function
'

Private Function KC34_StableFrameName(ByVal name As String, ByVal requested As String) As Boolean
    Dim suffix As String
    If Left$(requested, 9) <> "MF_FIELD_" Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(name, requested, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC34_StableFrameName = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If StrComp(Left$(name, Len(requested) + 1), requested & "_", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    suffix = Mid$(name, Len(requested) + 2)
    KC34_StableFrameName = (Len(suffix) > 0 And Not (suffix Like "*[!0-9]*"))
End Function


' v16.5.3-review: âñïîìîãàòåëüíûå ïðîöåäóðû ñîãëàñîâàííîé ìèãðàöèè ÊÝ.
Private Sub KC35_OpenBook(ByVal path As String, ByRef xl As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByRef owns As Boolean)
    Dim item As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(CStr(item.FullName), path, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = item: Exit For
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        owns = True
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False: xl.Visible = False
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, False)
    End If
    If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Êíèãà îòêðûòà òîëüêî äëÿ ÷òåíèÿ. Äëÿ ìèãðàöèè íóæíà çàïèñü: " & path
    If Not book.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Ñíà÷àëà ñîõðàíèòå ïîëüçîâàòåëüñêèå èçìåíåíèÿ ðàáî÷åé êíèãè."
End Sub

Private Function KC35_BackupBook(ByVal book As Object) As String
    Dim path As String, candidate As String, suffix As Long, fso As Object
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    path = CStr(book.FullName)
    candidate = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & _
        ".before-ke-rename_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & ".xlsm")
    Do While fso.FileExists(candidate)
        suffix = suffix + 1
        candidate = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & _
            ".before-ke-rename_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(suffix) & ".xlsm")
    Loop
    book.SaveCopyAs candidate
    If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3581, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà ðåçåðâíàÿ êîïèÿ êàðòû."
    KC35_BackupBook = candidate
End Function

Private Sub KC35_CheckLegacyMarkerFiles(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawingPath As String)
    Dim ws As Object, fso As Object, suffix As Variant
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Ìàðêåðû ÊÊ")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each suffix In Array("_markers.xlsx", "_hashes.xlsx", "_at_notes.xlsx")
        If fso.FileExists(drawingPath & CStr(suffix)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3582, , _
            "Ñíà÷àëà ïåðåíåñèòå ïðåæíèå ìàðêåðû â ëèñò «Ìàðêåðû ÊÊ» ïåðâûì ìàêðîñîì. Ñòàðûå âíåøíèå ôàéëû íå ïåðåïèñûâàþòñÿ."
    Next suffix
End Sub

Private Function KC35_Sheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal name As String) As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set KC35_Sheet = book.Worksheets(name)
End Function

Private Function KC35_GetRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim text As String, restored As Object, specific As Object
    On Error GoTo Unsupported
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, ann)
    If Len(text) > 0 Then
        Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
        If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text: Exit Function
    End If
    Set specific = ann.GetSpecificAnnotation
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, specific)
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
    If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text
Unsupported:
End Function

Private Function KC35_ResolveRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal text As String) As Object
    Dim bytes() As Byte, i As Long, state As Long, obj As Object
    On Error GoTo Unresolved
    If Len(text) = 0 Or Len(text) Mod 2 <> 0 Then Exit Function
    ReDim bytes(0 To Len(text) \ 2 - 1)
    For i = 0 To UBound(bytes)
        bytes(i) = CByte(CLng("&H" & Mid$(text, i * 2 + 1, 2)))
    Next i
    Set obj = model.Extension.GetObjectByPersistReference3(bytes, state)
    If state = 0 And Not obj Is Nothing Then Set KC35_ResolveRef = KC40_AsAnnotation(obj)
Unresolved:
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddSource(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal ann As Object, ByVal kind As Long, _
                          ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal view As Object, ByVal note As Object)
    Dim rec As Object, have As Object
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    For Each rec In result
        Set have = rec("Ann")
        If KC39_SameObject(have, ann) Then
            If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldSheet")), CStr(sheet.GetName), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Îäíà àííîòàöèÿ îáíàðóæåíà íà ðàçíûõ ëèñòàõ; ïåðåíîñ çàáëîêèðîâàí."
            If Not view Is Nothing Then
                If Len(CStr(rec("OldView"))) > 0 Then
                    If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldView")), CStr(view.GetName2), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Îäíà àííîòàöèÿ îáíàðóæåíà â ðàçíûõ âèäàõ; âëàäåëåö íå îïðåäåë¸í."
                End If
                Set rec("View") = view
                rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
            End If
            If Not note Is Nothing Then Set rec("Note") = note
            If CLng(rec("Type")) <> kind Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Íå ñîâïàë òèï ïîâòîðíîé àííîòàöèè."
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next rec
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Ann", ann: rec.Add "Sheet", sheet: rec.Add "View", view: rec.Add "Note", note
    rec.Add "Type", kind: rec.Add "OldSheet", CStr(sheet.GetName): rec.Add "OldName", Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    rec.Add "OldView", ""
    If Not view Is Nothing Then rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
    result.Add rec
End Sub

Private Function KC35_CollectSources(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sheets As Collection) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, sheet As Variant, sel As Object, obj As Object, ann As Object
    Dim view As Object, dimObj As Object, note As Object, nothingObj As Object, i As Long, kind As Long
    For Each sheet In sheets
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3583, , "Íå àêòèâèðîâàí èñõîäíûé ëèñò."
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        model.Extension.SelectAll
        Set sel = model.SelectionManager
        For i = 1 To sel.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1)
            kind = sel.GetSelectedObjectType3(i, -1)
            ' Ðàçìåðû (14) íèæå ñîáèðàþòñÿ òîëüêî èç âèäîâ. Çäåñü òîëüêî GDT/ñâàðêà.
            If kind = 13 Or kind = 38 Then
                Set obj = sel.GetSelectedObject6(i, -1)
                Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, kind, sheet, nothingObj, nothingObj
            End If
        Next i
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        Set view = drawing.GetFirstView
        Do While Not view Is Nothing
            Set dimObj = view.GetFirstDisplayDimension5
            Do While Not dimObj Is Nothing
                Set ann = dimObj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 14, sheet, view, nothingObj
                Set dimObj = dimObj.GetNext5
            Loop
            Set note = view.GetFirstNote
            Do While Not note Is Nothing
                Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 15, sheet, view, note
                Set note = note.GetNext
            Loop
            Set view = view.GetNextView
        Loop
    Next sheet
    Set KC35_CollectSources = result
End Function

' Íîðìàëèçàöèÿ è õåø ñîâïàäàþò ñ KC_IdentityRaw/StableHash ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà.
Private Function KC35_Normalize(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = LCase$(Trim$(text))
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(216), "d"): text = Replace(text, ChrW(248), "d")
    text = Replace(text, Chr(177), "+-"): text = Replace(text, ",", ".")
    Do While InStr(text, "  ") > 0: text = Replace(text, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC35_Normalize = text
End Function

Private Function KC35_Hash(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim a As Double, b As Double, code As Long, i As Long
    Const modulus As Double = 2147483647#
    a = 5381#: b = 7#
    For i = 1 To Len(text)
        code = AscW(Mid$(text, i, 1))
        If code < 0 Then code = code + 65536
        a = a * 33# + code: a = a - Int(a / modulus) * modulus
        b = b * 131# + code: b = b - Int(b / modulus) * modulus
    Next i
    KC35_Hash = Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(a)), 8) & Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(b)), 8)
End Function

Private Function KC35_SourceKey(ByVal model As Object, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal old As Boolean, ByVal extended As Boolean) As String
    Dim sheetName As String, sourceName As String, raw As String, ref As String, ann As Object, sheet As Object
    Set ann = rec("Ann"): Set sheet = rec("Sheet")
    If old Then
        sheetName = CStr(rec("OldSheet")): sourceName = CStr(rec("OldName"))
    Else
        sheetName = CStr(sheet.GetName): sourceName = Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    End If
    If Len(sourceName) = 0 Then Exit Function
    raw = "KC_ID2|" & CStr(rec("Type")) & "|" & KC35_Normalize(sheetName) & "|" & LCase$(Trim$(sourceName))
    If extended Then
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then Exit Function
        raw = raw & "|" & ref
    End If
    KC35_SourceKey = "KC_B2_" & KC35_Hash(raw)
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddKeyCandidate(ByVal index As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal extended As Boolean)
    Dim list As Collection, item As Object
    If Len(key) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not index.Exists(key) Then
        Set list = New Collection
        index.Add key, list
    End If
    Set list = index(key)
    Set item = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    item.Add "Source", rec: item.Add "Extended", extended
    list.Add item
End Sub

Private Function KC35_PlanBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, index As Object, used As Object, rec As Object, candidate As Object, plan As Object
    Dim name As String, key As String, note As Object, list As Collection, source As Object
    Set index = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In sources
        name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
        If Left$(name, 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(name, 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, False), rec, False
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, True), rec, True
        End If
    Next rec
    For Each rec In sources
        Set note = rec("Note")
        If Not note Is Nothing Then
            name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
            If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                "Íà ëèñòå " & rec("OldSheet") & " îñòàëñÿ êîîðäèíàòíûé êëþ÷ v15.12. Ñíà÷àëà îáíîâèòå áàëëîíû ðåæèìîì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà."
            If Left$(name, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then
                If Not index.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                    "Íå íàéäåí èñõîäíûé îáúåêò êëþ÷à " & name & " íà ëèñòå " & rec("OldSheet") & ". Ïåðåèìåíîâàíèå çàáëîêèðîâàíî."
                Set list = index(name)
                If list.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Íåîäíîçíà÷íûé êëþ÷ áàëëîíà " & name
                If used.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Íåñêîëüêî áàëëîíîâ èñïîëüçóþò êëþ÷ " & name
                used.Add name, True
                Set candidate = list(1): Set source = candidate("Source")
                If StrComp(CStr(source("OldSheet")), CStr(rec("OldSheet")), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Êëþ÷ áàëëîíà óêàçûâàåò íà èñõîäíûé îáúåêò äðóãîãî ëèñòà."
                Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                plan.Add "Ann", rec("Ann"): plan.Add "Source", source
                plan.Add "OldName", name: plan.Add "Extended", CBool(candidate("Extended"))
                plan.Add "NewName", "": plan.Add "TempName", ""
                result.Add plan
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    Set KC35_PlanBalloons = result
End Function

Private Function KC35_PlanMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, ws As Object, rec As Object, target As Object, ann As Object, owner As Object
    Dim row As Long, lastRow As Long, savedSheet As String, ref As String, hits As Long, plan As Object
    Dim affected As Boolean, expectedKind As String
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Ìàðêåðû ÊÊ")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result: Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Íåâåðíûé ôîðìàò èëè âëàäåëåö ëèñòà «Ìàðêåðû ÊÊ»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Ëèñò" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 4).Value2) <> "Âèä" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 5).Value2) <> "Èìÿ" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Èçìåíåíû çàãîëîâêè ëèñòà ìàðêåðîâ."
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    For row = 6 To lastRow
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
        affected = (KC21_MappedName(savedSheet, oldNames, newNames, total) <> savedSheet)
        ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        Set target = Nothing: Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
        If Len(ref) > 0 Then Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
        If Not target Is Nothing Then
            For Each rec In sources
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
            Next rec
        End If
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Íåîäíîçíà÷íûé âëàäåëåö ìàðêåðà â ñòðîêå " & row
        If hits = 0 And affected Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , _
            "Ìàðêåð, ñòðîêà " & row & ": íåò ïîäòâåðæä¸ííîé persistent reference. Ïåðåèìåíîâàíèå íå íà÷àòî; ïðîâåðüòå/ïåðåñîõðàíèòå ñïèñîê íà èñõîäíûõ ëèñòàõ."
        If hits = 1 Then
            expectedKind = ""
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then expectedKind = "DIM"
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then expectedKind = "NOTE"
            If CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> expectedKind Or Len(expectedKind) = 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Íå ñîâïàë òèï îáúåêòà ìàðêåðà, ñòðîêà " & row
            Set ann = owner("View")
            If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Íå íàéäåí âèä âëàäåëüöà ìàðêåðà, ñòðîêà " & row
            Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            plan.Add "Sheet", ws: plan.Add "Row", row: plan.Add "Owner", owner
            result.Add plan
        End If
    Next row
    Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result
End Function

Private Sub KC35_SetAnnName(ByVal ann As Object, ByVal name As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ann.SetName2 name
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        ann.SetName name
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíî èìÿ àííîòàöèè " & name
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_RekeyBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, source As Object, ann As Object, seen As Object, i As Long, key As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each plan In plans
        Set source = plan("Source")
        key = KC35_SourceKey(model, source, False, CBool(plan("Extended")))
        If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Íå âû÷èñëåí íîâûé êëþ÷ áàëëîíà."
        If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Ñîâïàëè íîâûå êëþ÷è ðàçíûõ áàëëîíîâ."
        seen.Add key, True: plan("NewName") = key
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann")
        plan("TempName") = "KC_RN_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("TempName"))
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann")
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("NewName"))
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreBalloonNames(ByVal plans As Collection) As Long
    Dim plan As Object, ann As Object, i As Long, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, "KC_RB_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("OldName"))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    KC35_RestoreBalloonNames = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteCell(ByVal cell As Object, ByVal text As String, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim rec As Object
    If cell.HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Ôîðìóëà â ñëóæåáíîé ÿ÷åéêå " & cell.address & "; çàìåíà çàïðåùåíà."
    If CStr(cell.Value2) = text Then Exit Sub
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Cell", cell: rec.Add "Formula", cell.formula
    changes.Add rec
    cell.Value2 = text
    If CStr(cell.Value2) <> text Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Íå ïîäòâåðæäåíà çàïèñü " & cell.address
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreCells(ByVal changes As Collection) As Long
    Dim i As Long, rec As Object, cell As Object, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For i = changes.count To 1 Step -1
        Set rec = changes(i): Set cell = rec("Cell"): Err.Clear
        cell.formula = rec("Formula")
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            errors = errors + 1
        ElseIf CStr(cell.formula) <> CStr(rec("Formula")) Then
            errors = errors + 1
        End If
    Next i
    KC35_RestoreCells = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteMarkerOwners(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, owner As Object, ws As Object, ann As Object, sheet As Object, view As Object
    Dim row As Long, ref As String, restored As Object
    For Each plan In plans
        Set owner = plan("Owner"): Set ws = plan("Sheet"): row = CLng(plan("Row"))
        Set ann = owner("Ann"): Set sheet = owner("Sheet"): Set view = owner("View")
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then
            ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
            Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
            If restored Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3588, , "Ïîòåðÿíà persistent reference ìàðêåðà."
            If Not (restored Is ann) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3588, , "Ìàðêåð ðàçðåøèëñÿ â äðóãîé îáúåêò."
        End If
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 3), CStr(sheet.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 4), CStr(view.GetName2), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 5), CStr(ann.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 9), ref, changes
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_ValidateRaw(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Íåò ëèñòà «Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Ëèñò" Or CStr(ws.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "×åðò¸æíûé âèä" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Èçìåíåíà ñõåìà èñõîäíûõ äàííûõ W:X."
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_MapRaw(ByVal book As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, _
                       ByVal total As Long, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, row As Long, old As String, mapped As String, oldView As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà")
    For row = 2 To ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        old = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        mapped = KC21_MappedName(old, oldNames, newNames, total)
        If mapped <> old Then
            oldView = CStr(ws.cells(row, 24).Value2)
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 23), mapped, changes
            ' Ìåíÿåì òîëüêî èìÿ ñëóæåáíîãî âèäà ëèñòà, à íå ïðîèçâîëüíóþ ïîäïèñü âèäà.
            If oldView = old Then KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 24), mapped, changes
        End If
    Next row
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_Setting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal key As String, ByVal value As String)
    KC14_SaveSetting drawing, key, value
    If KC14_Setting(drawing, key, ChrW(1)) <> value Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3590, , "Íå ñîõðàí¸í ïàðàìåòð " & key
End Sub


' v16.5.3-review: îòñóòñòâóþùèå ëèñòû è ïîòåðÿííûå ïðèâÿçêè ïðîâåðÿþòñÿ ÄÎ SetName.
Private Function KC36_IsRegular(ByVal name As String) As Boolean
    KC36_IsRegular = (RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name))
End Function

Private Function KC36_CurrentNames(ByVal drawing As Object) As Object
    Dim result As Object, names As Variant, i As Long, first As Long, last As Long
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Íåò ñïèñêà òåêóùèõ ëèñòîâ."
    For i = first To last: result.Add CStr(names(i)), True: Next i
    Set KC36_CurrentNames = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_CheckReview(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If Left$(CStr(ws.name), 9) = "ÊÝ_Àðõèâ_" Then
            If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1" Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Íåèçâåñòíûé ôîðìàò àðõèâà ÊÝ: " & ws.name
            If CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2) <> CStr(book.Worksheets("ÎÊ_Ñëóæåáíîå").Range("B2").Value2) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Àðõèâ îòíîñèòñÿ ê äðóãîìó ÷åðòåæó: " & ws.name
            If Not KD_CompletedArchive(ws) And UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2))) <> "ÏÐÎÂÅÐÅÍÎ" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Óäàëåíèå ÊÝ íå çàâåðøåíî. Ñíà÷àëà âûïîëíèòå ðåæèì 1 ïåðâîãî ìàêðîñà. Àðõèâ: " & ws.name
            End If
        End If
    Next ws
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_AddMissing(ByVal missing As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal name As String)
    If KC36_IsRegular(name) And Not current.Exists(name) Then missing(name) = True
End Sub

Private Function KC36_Missing(ByVal book As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal known As String) As Object
    Dim result As Object, ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, count As Long, value As Variant, owner As String
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ' Registry SheetsKnown is only advisory. It may belong to a deleted earlier workbook.
    ' Actual historical ownership comes from the CURRENT validated workbook, not the registry.
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Äàííûå èç ÷åðòåæà")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Ñëèøêîì áîëüøîé ñïèñîê èñõîäíûõ äàííûõ."
        For row = 2 To last: KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2): Next row
    End If
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Ìàðêåðû ÊÊ")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Ñëèøêîì áîëüøîé ñïèñîê ìàðêåðîâ."
